# AGENTS.md — Granny POV 3D Cartoon Studio Playbook

> **Episode Producers / Script Generators:** Read **`EPISODE_PRODUCTION_GUIDE.md`** before generating any JSON episode! It contains the full `SET_MAP` doorway chains, all 28 SFX/BGM keys (including `SoundEffects/backroundMusics/`), and the mandatory kids-comedy pacing checklist.

This repository is a zero-build Three.js (`v0.170.0` via importmap) first-person **POV Kids-Comedy 3D Cartoon Studio** inside the Granny 1 House (`./Assets/granny-1-house/source/GrannyHouse.fbx`), with deterministic 60Hz animation, Mixamo retargeting (`Granny` + `Grandpa`), procedural 3D comedy props + 2D comic thought bubbles, a Web Audio SFX/BGM mixer (`./SoundEffects` + `./SoundEffects/backroundMusics`), and a GitHub Actions cloud video exporter (`16:9` widescreen & `9:16` Shorts).

## Mandatory Kids-Comedy Pacing Rules (For Every New Episode)

1. **No beat over 2–2.5s without movement ("Zero Dead Air"):** Never hold the camera on a bobbing prop or idle pose for 3–5s. If a prop pops out, immediately send it across the room (`to` + `travelDuration`), bonk someone, or grab it (`player_hold`).
2. **Show cause-and-effect visually (no shadowboxing thin air):** Never trigger `punch`/`hit` from 3m away in a doorway. Bounce the prop into Granny's face *first*, or sprint Granny (`run2`) within `1.4m` of her target before swinging.
3. **End on motion, not a still pose:** End the final `2–3s` before `fade` (`to: 1`) on Granny lunging toward the camera (`run2` + `jumpscare`) or Grandpa panicking and sprinting out of the room while Granny chases him right behind.

## Architecture Overview

- `index.html` — Studio UI shell, importmap (`three@0.170.0`), `#stageContainer`, `#fadeOverlay`, and `?headless=1` export mode.
- `src/config.js` — `CONFIG`, `ASSET_MANIFEST`, `SFX_MANIFEST`, verified indoor `SET_MAP` waypoints, `GRANDPA_KEY_POV_44S` (44s default episode), and `JUMP_RUN_WALK_EPISODE` (6s).
- `src/assetLoader.js` — `loadHouse()` with 0.4m indoor A* navigation (`findPath`, `isPointInsideWalkable`, `clampCameraPosition`), `loadCharacterTemplate()`, `instantiateCharacter()` (`playAnim`, `faceTarget`, `lookAtPlayer`, `showBubble`, `setProp`).
- `src/animationLibrary.js` — Mixamo `.fbx` template extraction, root-motion stripping, jump arc extraction (`0.32m` cap), and rest-pose quaternion retargeting (`AnimationLibrary`).
- `src/playerCamera.js` — First-person POV controller (`PlayerCamera`): eye-level walking (`1.50m`) with head-bob, whip-pan `lookAt` + tracking, `nod_yes` / `shake_no` / `shake_panic` / `peek_lean` gestures, crouch (`0.42m`), bottom-right FPS held-item viewmodel (`holdProp`, `useHeldProp`), fade-to-black, wall clamping, and `16:9` / `9:16` aspect toggling + Orbit calibrator toggle.
- `src/comedyProps.js` — `ThoughtBubbleCache` (`key`, `question`, `exclamation`, `zzz`, `angry`, `laugh`, `food`, `heart`) and `ComedyPropManager` (`golden_key`, `padlock`, `treasure_chest`, `emoji_ball`, `toy_blaster`, `cookie_jar`).
- `src/sfxEngine.js` — Web Audio mixer (`SfxEngine`): looping BGM (`fluffing_a_duck`, `monkeys_spinning_monkeys`, `run_amok` in `SoundEffects/backroundMusics/`, plus `granny_ambience`, `granny_good_part`, `granny_chase`), `cutBgm` silence ducking, movement-synced footsteps, whip-pan/bubble/pickup/action foley, 1–2s gap pacer, and deterministic offline capture for FFmpeg.
- `src/episodeRunner.js` — Deterministic 60Hz multi-skit timeline (`EpisodeRunner`) compiling `skits[].beats` (`at` / `after`) and legacy `actions`.
- `src/studioUI.js` & `src/main.js` — Director controls, 28-clip Soundboard, Waypoint Calibrator (`WASD + Q/E`), Live Multi-Skit JSON Editor, and `window.__GRANNY_STUDIO__` headless export hooks.
- `scripts/export-video.mjs` & `.github/workflows/export-video.yml` — Headless Playwright (SwiftShader WebGL) + FFmpeg video/BGM/ducking/SFX pipeline auto-publishing `.mp4`/`.webm` + `export-summary.json` to the `latest-export` GitHub Release.

## Rigging & House Rules (Must Preserve)

1. **Granny Bind Matrix**: `GrannyTest6.fbx` stores Identity `bindMatrix` on its `SkinnedMesh`. Keep `fixBindMatrixToWorld: true` (`AttachedBindMode` with `node.bindMatrix.copy(node.matrixWorld)`) before normalization. Never switch Granny to `DetachedBindMode`.
2. **Textured Materials**: FBX `MeshPhongMaterial` instances can have dark diffuse multipliers (`#242424`, `#3c3c3c`). `configureMaterialsAndShadows()` resets `material.color` to `0xffffff` whenever `material.map` is present.
3. **Indoor Waypoints (`SET_MAP`)**: Always reference `SET_MAP` waypoint names in episode scripts rather than raw coordinates. Avoid the unroofed front porch (`x = -2.4..+1.8, z = +1.6..+5.8`) and cellar stair hole (`x = +2.4, z = -0.2..+2.8`).

## Multi-Skit POV Episode JSON Schema

```json
{
  "title": "My Funny Skit",
  "aspect": "16:9",
  "playerSpawn": "back_hall",
  "playerFacing": "back_room_east",
  "cast": [
    { "id": "grandpa", "asset": "grandpa", "spawn": "living_room", "facing": "living_room_entry" },
    { "id": "granny", "asset": "granny", "spawn": "kitchen", "facing": "dining_nook" }
  ],
  "props": [
    { "id": "chest", "type": "treasure_chest", "spawn": "chest_spot" },
    { "id": "golden_key", "type": "golden_key", "spawn": "key_spot", "yOffset": 0.82, "spin": true, "float": true }
  ],
  "skits": [
    {
      "name": "Skit 1",
      "beats": [
        { "at": 0.0, "type": "bgm", "sound": "granny_ambience", "volume": 0.22 },
        { "at": 0.2, "type": "player_walk", "to": "living_room_entry", "duration": 3.0 },
        { "after": 3.2, "type": "player_look", "target": "grandpa", "duration": 0.18, "track": true },
        { "after": 0.1, "type": "char_face", "actor": "grandpa", "followPlayer": true },
        { "after": 0.1, "type": "char_bubble", "actor": "grandpa", "icon": "question", "duration": 1.8 },
        { "after": 0.2, "type": "sfx", "sound": "vine_boom", "cutBgm": true, "cutBgmDuration": 1.2 }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

### Supported Beat Types
- **Player POV**: `player_walk` (`to`, `duration`, `lookAhead`), `player_look` (`target`, `duration`, `track`), `player_gesture` (`gesture`: `nod_yes` | `shake_no` | `shake_panic` | `peek_lean`, `duration`), `player_crouch` (`crouched`, `duration`), `player_hold` (`prop`, `use`), `fade` (`to`, `duration`).
- **Characters**: `char_move` (`actor`, `to`, `anim`, `duration`, `speed`), `char_anim` (`actor`, `anim`, `speed`, `loop`), `char_face` (`actor`, `target`, `followPlayer`), `char_bubble` (`actor`, `icon`: `key` | `question` | `exclamation` | `zzz` | `angry` | `laugh` | `food` | `heart`, `duration`), `char_prop` (`actor`, `prop`).
- **Props & Audio**: `prop_action` (`prop`, `action`: `show` | `hide` | `pickup` | `open` | `close` | `pop_out` | `bounce` | `stop`, `waypoint`, `yOffset`), `bgm` (`sound`, `volume`), `stop_bgm`, `sfx` (`sound`, `volume`, `pitch`, `cutBgm`, `cutBgmDuration`).

## Cloud Video Rendering (GitHub Actions)

Render any preset or custom JSON to `.mp4`/`.webm` in GitHub's cloud runners without using local CPU/GPU:
```bash
gh workflow run export-video.yml --ref main -f preset=grandpa_key_pov_44s -f fps=30 -f sfx_pacer=true -f format=mp4
```
Completed videos are auto-published to the `latest-export` GitHub Release.

===== FILE: EPISODE_PRODUCTION_GUIDE.md =====

# EPISODE_PRODUCTION_GUIDE.md — Granny POV Comedy Short Playbook

Use this guide whenever you write, generate, or render a new **Granny POV 3D Cartoon Episode** (`16:9` YouTube POV or `9:16` Shorts). Every rule here comes from real rendered output audits and maps directly to `src/config.js`, `src/episodeRunner.js`, `src/playerCamera.js`, `src/comedyProps.js`, `src/sfxEngine.js`, and `scripts/export-video.mjs`.

---

## 1. Where Everything Lives in the Repo

| What you need | Where to find it | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Waypoints, Cast, SFX/BGM Manifest & Presets** | `src/config.js` | `SET_MAP`, `SFX_MANIFEST`, `ASSET_MANIFEST`, `GRANDPA_KEY_POV_44S`, `FLOOR_IS_LAVA_4M`, `JUMP_RUN_WALK_EPISODE` |
| **Background Music (Looping Comedy/Chase BGM)** | `SoundEffects/backroundMusics/` + `SoundEffects/*.mp3` | Registered in `SFX_MANIFEST` with `category: "bgm"` — auto-loops in browser & FFmpeg |
| **22 Sound Effects (Impacts, Voices, UI, Cuts)** | `SoundEffects/*.mp3` | Registered in `SFX_MANIFEST` in `src/config.js` |
| **3D House & Character Models + Mixamo Clips** | `Assets/` | `GrannyHouse.fbx`, `GrannyTest6.fbx`, `grandpach3.fbx`, `Assets/AnimationTemplates/*.fbx` |
| **Timeline Engine & Beats** | `src/episodeRunner.js` | Compiles `skits[].beats` (`at` / `after`), `await: true` / `sync: "sequential"` barriers, auto stair routing, jump-arc hops, 1.3m personal space |
| **POV Camera & FPS Viewmodel** | `src/playerCamera.js` | Clamps pitch `[-45°, +35°]`, stair vertical clamping, jump-arc hops, depth-cleared `viewmodelScene`, gestures, fade |
| **Props & Thought Bubbles** | `src/comedyProps.js` | 6 props + 8 icon bubbles (`Emoji Ball` double-sided + camera yaw-tracking) |
| **Cloud Video Exporter** | `.github/workflows/export-video.yml` & `scripts/export-video.mjs` | Renders 30fps `.mp4`/`.webm` + mixed audio in GitHub Actions → `latest-export` release |

---

## 2. The 3 Mandatory Kids-Comedy Pacing Rules

Kids' attention drops within **3 seconds** if nothing changes visually. Bake these three rules into **every** episode JSON script you create:

### Rule 1: No Beat Over 2–2.5 Seconds Without Movement ("Zero Dead Air")
- **Never** leave the camera staring at a bobbing prop or stationary character for 3–5 seconds.
- **Every `1.5–2.5s`**, change something on screen: a whip-pan (`player_look`), camera walk (`player_walk`), crouch/peek (`player_crouch` / `peek_lean`), prop flying across the room (`pop_out` with `to` + `travelDuration`), pickup (`player_hold`), or a character moving/reacting (`char_move` / `char_anim` + `char_bubble`).
- If a prop pops out of a box or chest, **immediately** send it flying across the room (`to` waypoint), bonk a character with it, or grab it into the player's hand within `1.5–2.0s`.

### Rule 2: Always Show Cause-and-Effect Visually (No Shadowboxing Thin Air)
- **Never** trigger `punch` or `hit` while a character is 3+ meters away in a doorway with nobody in reach—it reads as shadowboxing thin air and breaks the comedy logic.
- **Before any angry swing/reaction:**
  - Either have the gag prop (`emoji_ball`, `cookie_jar`, `toy_blaster`) **bounce/fly right to Granny's position first** (`bonk` + `vine_boom`), **OR**
  - Have Granny **`run2` sprint right up to the target (`<= 1.4m` away)** *before* triggering `angry` + `punch` / `jump`.
- If Granny hears a noise while you're hiding (`!`), have her **stop, turn to face the room (`char_face` + `look`)**, show `?` when she doesn't spot you, and *then* leave—never make her shout `"I see you!"` while walking away backwards.

### Rule 3: End on Motion, Not a Static Pose
- **Never** end an episode with a character repeating a stationary `terrified` or `idle` crouch while explosion/chase SFX play offscreen.
- **Best high-retention endings (last `2–3s` into `fade`):**
  1. **Camera Lunge:** Granny switches to `run2`, sprints straight toward the player/camera (`char_face: followPlayer: true`) with `jumpscare` / `granny_laugh` + `shake_panic` right as `fade` goes to `1`.
  2. **Slapstick Chase Exit:** Grandpa panics and sprints (`run2`) out through `doorway_living` → `back_room_east` while Granny chases right behind him (`0.4s` later, `run2`) as the camera pans with them and fades to black!

---

## 3. Audio, Looping BGM (`SoundEffects/backroundMusics/`), & SFX Guide

### Background Music Tracks (`category: "bgm"` — Automatically Looped)
Use `{ "at": ..., "type": "bgm", "sound": "<key>", "volume": 0.24 }`. Switching to a new `bgm` beat cleanly replaces the active track, and `SfxEngine` + `export-video.mjs` (`-stream_loop -1`) automatically loop every track seamlessly.

| `sound` key | File Path | Mood / Best Use | Credits (Keep for descriptions if needed) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **`fluffing_a_duck`** | `SoundEffects/backroundMusics/Fluffing-a-Duck(chosic.com).mp3` | **Sneaky / Quirky Setup** (sneaking, plotting, goofy discovery) | Kevin MacLeod (`incompetech.com`), CC BY 3.0 |
| **`monkeys_spinning_monkeys`** | `SoundEffects/backroundMusics/Monkeys-Spinning-Monkeys(chosic.com).mp3` | **Upbeat Slapstick / Comedy Hijinks** (pranks, bouncing props, silly chases) | Kevin MacLeod (`incompetech.com`), CC BY 3.0 |
| **`run_amok`** | `SoundEffects/backroundMusics/Run-Amok(chosic.com).mp3` | **Frantic High-Speed Chase** (climax sprint, Granny bursting in, chaos finale) | Kevin MacLeod (`incompetech.com`), CC BY 3.0 |
| `granny_ambience` | `SoundEffects/granny-ambience.mp3` | Creepy low tension intro / suspense | Built-in |
| `granny_good_part` | `SoundEffects/granny-ambience-but-its-only-the-good-part.mp3` | Suspenseful hallway sneak / hide-and-seek | Built-in |
| `granny_chase` | `SoundEffects/granny-chase-music.mp3` | Intense Granny horror-chase sting | Built-in |

> **Pro Comedy Audio Trick (`cutBgm`):** On a sudden shock or punchline, add `"cutBgm": true, "cutBgmDuration": 1.1` to an `sfx` beat (`vine_boom`, `bonk`, `granny_i_see_you`, `discord`). It mutes the BGM into dead silence for 1.1 seconds so the gag lands 3x harder, then brings the music right back!

### Full 22-Clip SFX Catalog (`SFX_MANIFEST`)
Sync SFX directly to visible actions (`1.2s–1.85s` rhythm; avoid overlapping voices):

- **Impacts & Slapstick:** `bonk`, `vine_boom`, `explode`, `explode_meme`
- **Pops, Alerts & UI:** `pop`, `ding`, `discord`, `glitch`, `checkmark`, `click`
- **Comedy Payoffs:** `tada`, `kaching`, `rizz`, `aww`, `eat`
- **Voices & Horror Stings:** `granny_i_see_you`, `granny_laugh`, `jumpscare`
- **Movement & Cuts:** `swoosh` (whip-pan camera turn), `whoosh` (fast movement), `squidward_walk` (comic footsteps), `typing`

---

## 4. Indoor Stage Map & Safe Doorway Routing (`SET_MAP`)

**NEVER** hardcode raw `(x, y, z)` coordinates in episode JSON—always use `SET_MAP` names, and **ALWAYS** list doorway waypoints in `via: [...]` when moving between rooms so the camera and characters pass cleanly through doorway centers without clipping wall corners!

### Safe Room-to-Room `via` Chains & Vertical Stair Routing
- **Back Hall ↔ Living Room (Grandpa's Room):**
  - `back_hall` → `back_room_east` → **`doorway_living`** → **`living_room_entry`** → `living_room_view`
- **Foyer ↔ Dining Room / Kitchen:**
  - `front_door_view` → `stairs_bottom` → `dining_corner` → `dining_table_front` → `kitchen`
- **Back Hall ↔ Dining Room / Kitchen:**
  - `back_hall` → **`doorway_dining`** → `dining_center` → `kitchen_lane` → `kitchen`
- **Ground Floor ↔ Upstairs (Automatic Staircase Routing):**
  - Any cross-floor `char_move` or `player_walk` automatically routes through `stairs_bottom` (`-1.9, 0.0, 4.0`) ↔ `stairs_middle` (`-1.9, 1.54, 2.5`) ↔ `stairs_top` (`-1.9, 2.85, 1.25`) ↔ `upstairs_landing` (`-1.9, 2.85, 0.95`) with vertical speed clamping (`<= 1.75 m/s` actors, `<= 1.55 m/s` player) so nobody flies diagonally through floors or ceilings.
- **Upstairs North Doorway & East Wing:**
  - `upstairs_landing` → `up_hall_north_door` (`-1.4, 2.85, -0.6`) → `up_hall_north` (`-1.4, 2.85, -2.4`) → `up_bed_north` / `upstairs_east_north` (`1.5, 2.85, -1.8`)

### Elevated Furniture Waypoints (`elevated: true`) & Jump Arcs
Targets with `elevated: true` in `SET_MAP` automatically route to their ground `approach` waypoint first, then perform a parabolic jump-arc hop (`jump` animation for characters) onto the elevated surface—or hop directly between adjacent surfaces (`< 2.2m` apart on the same floor):
- **Ground Floor Elevated:** `dining_table_top` (`y=1.11`), `dining_table_top_west` (`y=1.11`), `kitchen_table_top` (`y=1.13`), `kitchen_counter_top` (`y=1.13`), `back_bed_top` (`y=0.98`), `living_room_sofa` (`y=0.47`), `living_room_shelf_top` (`y=0.72`)
- **Upstairs Elevated:** `up_bed_top` (`y=3.83`), `up_bed_top_west` (`y=3.83`), `upstairs_west_desk_top` (`y=3.54`), `upstairs_east_bed_top` (`y=3.54`)

### Timeline Synchronization (`await: true` & `sync: "sequential"`)
- Add `"await": true` (or `"sync": "sequential"`) to any `char_move`, `player_walk`, `char_anim`, `player_look`, or `player_gesture` beat to **halt timeline advancement** until that character/camera movement or one-shot animation finishes before dispatching the next beat.
- Use `"after": <seconds>` for delta timing relative to the **previous beat** (or immediately after the previous `await: true` beat finishes). Keep any single `await: true` walk segment under `~2.2s` (or fire reaction beats right before it) to satisfy Rule 1 (`<= 2.5s` pacing).

### Key Waypoints Reference (`src/config.js`)
- **Living Room (Grandpa's Room):** `doorway_living` (`4.4, 0, -0.7`), `living_room_entry` (`4.7, 0, 0.2`), `living_room_view` (`5.4, 0, 0.6`), `living_room` (`5.4, 0, 2.0`), `living_room_ball_bounce` (`5.5, 0, 1.7`), `granny_living_burst` (`4.2, 0, -0.7`), `chest_spot` (`6.2, 0, 2.6`), `chest_padlock` (`5.95, 0, 2.35`), `grandpa_chest_side` (`4.3, 0, 2.5`), `grandpa_step_back` (`4.0, 0, 2.9`)
- **Back Hallway & Bedroom Stairs:** `back_hall` (`0.0, 0, -2.2`), `back_hall_left` (`-0.8, 0, -3.5`), `back_hall_right` (`0.6, 0, -2.6`), `hallway` (`0.8, 0, -2.4`), `back_room_east` (`3.2, 0, -1.8`), `under_bed` (`-3.2, 0, -2.8`), `stairs_bottom` (`-1.9, 0, 4.0`), `stairs_middle` (`-1.9, 1.54, 2.5`), `stairs_top` (`-1.9, 2.85, 1.25`), `upstairs_landing` (`-1.9, 2.85, 0.95`)
- **Dining Room & Kitchen:** `doorway_dining` (`-3.6, 0, -1.2`), `dining_center` (`-3.6, 0, 0.4`), `hide_table` (`-4.2, 0, 2.3`), `kitchen_lane` (`-5.8, 0, 0.8`), `kitchen` (`-6.4, 0, 1.4`), `key_spot` (`-7.35, 1.13, 1.4` — elevated counter top!)
- **Avoid:** The unroofed front porch (`x = -2.4..+1.8, z = +1.6..+5.8`) and cellar stair hole (`x = +2.4, z = -0.2..+2.8`).

---

## 5. Cast, Animations, Props & Thought Bubbles

- **Characters (`cast[]`):** `granny` (`1.65m`), `grandpa` (`1.72m`). Always spawn characters facing into the open room (`facing: "living_room_view"`, etc.), and use `char_face` with `followPlayer: true, instant: true` before dialogue/reaction beats.
- **Animations (`anim`):**
  - **Looping locomotion (use in `char_move`):** `walk`, `run`, `run2` (fast sprint), `sneak`, `granny_walk` (Granny only). `EpisodeRunner` automatically enforces `LoopRepeat` and crossfades back to `endAnim` (`idle` by default) on arrival.
  - **Emotes & actions (use in `char_anim`):** `idle`, `laugh` (`loop: true`), `terrified` (`loop: true`), `panic` (`loop: true`, sped-up `Terrified.fbx` for instant hazard reactions), `sit` (`loop: true`), `jump` (`loop: false`), `punch` (`loop: false`), plus Granny-only `look`, `hit`, `look_bed`.
- **Thought Bubbles (`icon`):** `key`, `question`, `exclamation`, `zzz`, `angry`, `laugh`, `food`, `heart`. Keep duration `1.4s–2.4s`. Viewport NDC clamping prevents top-edge cropping automatically.
- **Comedy Props (`type`):** `golden_key`, `padlock`, `treasure_chest`, `emoji_ball`, `toy_blaster`, `cookie_jar`, `delivery_box` (`open` opens flaps), `red_button` (`press` slams cap), `rising_lava` (`rise_to` with `targetY` & `duration` animates rising molten lava).
  - Use `player_hold` (`{ "prop": "golden_key" }`) for the non-clipping bottom-right FPS viewmodel, `{ "prop": "golden_key", "use": true }` to poke/activate, and `{ "prop": null }` to clear.
  - Gestures (`player_gesture`): `nod_yes`, `shake_no`, `shake_panic`, `shake_earthquake` (sustained `duration` camera rumble), `peek_lean`. Pitch is clamped `[-45°, +35°]`, and elevated waypoints (`y > 0.2`, e.g. `key_spot`) automatically target the prop rather than the ceiling.

---

## 6. Copy-Paste Episode JSON Blueprint (Fast 30s Comedy Chase Example)

Notice how every beat stays under `2.5s`, every reaction shows visual cause-and-effect, BGM escalates from `fluffing_a_duck` → `monkeys_spinning_monkeys` → `run_amok`, and the ending runs straight into a motion chase fade:

```json
{
  "title": "Granny's Stolen Cookie Jar (30s POV)",
  "aspect": "9:16",
  "playerSpawn": "doorway_dining",
  "playerFacing": "kitchen_lane",
  "cast": [
    { "id": "grandpa", "asset": "grandpa", "spawn": "dining_center", "facing": "doorway_dining" },
    { "id": "granny", "asset": "granny", "spawn": "back_hall_left", "facing": "doorway_dining" }
  ],
  "props": [
    { "id": "jar", "type": "cookie_jar", "spawn": "key_spot", "yOffset": 0.02, "float": false }
  ],
  "skits": [
    {
      "name": "Heist & Chase",
      "beats": [
        { "at": 0.0, "type": "bgm", "sound": "fluffing_a_duck", "volume": 0.24 },
        { "at": 0.0, "type": "fade", "to": 0, "duration": 0.35 },
        { "at": 0.2, "type": "char_face", "actor": "grandpa", "followPlayer": true, "instant": true },
        { "at": 0.3, "type": "char_bubble", "actor": "grandpa", "icon": "food", "duration": 2.0 },
        { "at": 0.4, "type": "sfx", "sound": "pop", "volume": 0.78 },
        { "at": 1.6, "type": "player_gesture", "gesture": "nod_yes", "duration": 0.7 },
        { "at": 2.2, "type": "player_walk", "via": ["dining_center"], "to": "kitchen", "duration": 2.1 },
        { "at": 4.3, "type": "player_look", "target": "jar", "duration": 0.2 },
        { "at": 4.7, "type": "prop_action", "prop": "jar", "action": "pickup" },
        { "at": 4.7, "type": "player_hold", "prop": "cookie_jar" },
        { "at": 4.8, "type": "sfx", "sound": "kaching", "volume": 0.8 },
        { "at": 5.3, "type": "player_look", "target": "dining_center", "duration": 0.25 },
        { "at": 5.6, "type": "char_move", "actor": "granny", "via": ["doorway_dining"], "to": "dining_center", "anim": "walk", "duration": 2.0 },
        { "at": 6.4, "type": "player_look", "target": "granny", "duration": 0.2, "track": true },
        { "at": 7.6, "type": "char_face", "actor": "granny", "followPlayer": true, "instant": true },
        { "at": 7.6, "type": "char_bubble", "actor": "granny", "icon": "angry", "duration": 2.0 },
        { "at": 7.6, "type": "sfx", "sound": "vine_boom", "volume": 0.9, "cutBgm": true, "cutBgmDuration": 1.1 },
        { "at": 8.7, "type": "bgm", "sound": "run_amok", "volume": 0.26 },
        { "at": 8.8, "type": "char_move", "actor": "granny", "to": "kitchen_lane", "anim": "run2", "duration": 1.4 },
        { "at": 10.2, "type": "char_face", "actor": "granny", "followPlayer": true, "instant": true },
        { "at": 10.2, "type": "char_anim", "actor": "granny", "anim": "punch", "loop": false },
        { "at": 10.5, "type": "sfx", "sound": "bonk", "volume": 0.9 },
        { "at": 10.5, "type": "player_gesture", "gesture": "shake_panic", "duration": 0.7 },
        { "at": 11.1, "type": "char_move", "actor": "grandpa", "via": ["doorway_dining"], "to": "back_hall", "anim": "run2", "duration": 2.2 },
        { "at": 11.4, "type": "char_move", "actor": "granny", "via": ["doorway_dining"], "to": "back_hall", "anim": "run2", "duration": 2.2 },
        { "at": 11.5, "type": "player_look", "target": "granny", "duration": 0.25, "track": true },
        { "at": 11.6, "type": "sfx", "sound": "granny_laugh", "volume": 0.85 },
        { "at": 12.6, "type": "fade", "to": 1, "duration": 0.9 }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

---

## 7. Pre-Render Checklist & Cloud Rendering

Before rendering a new episode JSON:
1. **No dead air > 2.5s:** Scan `beats` timestamps — every `2.5s` window should have movement, camera change, prop flight/pickup, or character action.
2. **Doorway-centered paths:** Every cross-room `player_walk` or `char_move` includes `doorway_living` / `doorway_dining` in `via`.
3. **Close-range cause-and-effect:** Any `punch` / `hit` happens within `1.4m` of its target or right after a prop impact.
4. **Motion ending:** Final `2s` before `fade` has active `run2` chase / lunge, never a frozen pose.
5. **Cloud render command (zero local CPU/GPU encoding):**
   ```bash
   gh workflow run export-video.yml --ref main \
     -f preset=custom_json \
     -f custom_json="$(cat path/to/my-episode.json)" \
     -f aspect=9:16 \
     -f fps=30 \
     -f sfx_pacer=true \
     -f format=mp4
   ```
   Rendered `.mp4` + `export-summary.json` are published automatically to the `latest-export` GitHub Release.


================================================================
===== FILE: .github/workflows/export-video.yml =====
================================================================
name: Export POV Episode (Cloud Render)

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      preset:
        description: "Episode preset to render"
        type: choice
        default: "grandpa_key_pov_44s"
        options:
          - grandpa_key_pov_44s
          - floor_is_lava_4m
          - jump_run_walk_6s
          - custom_json
      episode_json:
        description: "Custom Episode JSON (used when preset is custom_json, or overrides preset if provided)"
        type: string
        required: false
        default: ""
      aspect:
        description: "Aspect ratio override (auto uses the episode's own aspect)"
        type: choice
        default: "auto"
        options:
          - auto
          - "16:9"
          - "9:16"
      fps:
        description: "Output frame rate"
        type: choice
        default: "30"
        options:
          - "24"
          - "30"
          - "60"
      sfx_pacer:
        description: "Enable 1-2s Cartoon SFX Pacer"
        type: boolean
        default: true
      format:
        description: "Output video format"
        type: choice
        default: "mp4"
        options:
          - mp4
          - webm
          - both

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  render-pov:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 90

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js 22
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: "22"

      - name: Install FFmpeg
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y ffmpeg

      - name: Cache Playwright Chromium
        id: playwright-cache
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: ~/.cache/ms-playwright
          key: ${{ runner.os }}-playwright-chromium

      - name: Install Playwright & Three.js (ephemeral)
        run: |
          npm install --no-save playwright three@0.170.0
          npx playwright install chromium
          npx playwright install-deps chromium

      - name: Render 3D POV Episode in Cloud (SwiftShader WebGL + FFmpeg)
        env:
          EPISODE_JSON: ${{ inputs.episode_json }}
        run: |
          ASPECT_ARG=""
          if [ "${{ inputs.aspect }}" != "auto" ]; then
            ASPECT_ARG="--aspect ${{ inputs.aspect }}"
          fi
          node scripts/export-video.mjs \
            --preset "${{ inputs.preset }}" \
            --fps "${{ inputs.fps }}" \
            --pacer "${{ inputs.sfx_pacer }}" \
            --format "${{ inputs.format }}" \
            $ASPECT_ARG \
            --out-dir output

      - name: Upload Exported Video Artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: granny-pov-${{ inputs.preset }}-${{ github.run_number }}
          path: |
            output/*.mp4
            output/*.webm
            output/export-summary.json
          retention-days: 30

      - name: Publish Direct Video Download (GitHub Release)
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
        run: |
          shopt -s nullglob
          FILES=(output/*.mp4 output/*.webm output/export-summary.json)
          TAG="latest-export"
          if gh release view "$TAG" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            gh release upload "$TAG" "${FILES[@]}" --clobber
          else
            gh release create "$TAG" "${FILES[@]}" \
              --title "Latest Rendered Granny POV Exports" \
              --notes "Auto-published cloud video exports from Granny POV 3D Cartoon Studio."
          fi

      - name: Write Workflow Summary
        run: |
          node -e '
            const fs = require("fs");
            const s = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync("output/export-summary.json", "utf8"));
            const repo = process.env.GITHUB_REPOSITORY;
            const lines = [
              "## Granny POV 3D Cartoon Studio — Cloud Export Complete",
              "",
              `- **Episode:** ${s.title}`,
              `- **Duration:** ${s.duration.toFixed(2)}s @ ${s.fps}fps (${s.totalFrames} frames, ${s.width}x${s.height} [${s.aspectKey}])`,
              `- **Audio Mixed:** ${s.bgmCount} BGM segments + ${s.sfxCount} SFX events`,
              ...s.files.map(f => `- **Direct Download:** https://github.com/${repo}/releases/download/latest-export/${f.file} (${f.sizeMb} MB)`),
              "",
              "You can also download the zipped archive from **Artifacts** below."
            ];
            fs.appendFileSync(process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY, lines.join("\n") + "\n");
          '


================================================================

            <div data-tab-panel="director" class="card-stack">
              <div class="card">

===== FILE: README.md =====
================================================================
# Granny POV 3D Cartoon Studio

A zero-build Three.js (`v0.170.0`) first-person **Granny POV Kids-Comedy 3D Cartoon Studio** set inside the Granny 1 House (`./Assets/granny-1-house/source/GrannyHouse.fbx`), featuring Mixamo-retargeted Granny & Grandpa rigs, procedural 3D comedy props & 2D thought bubbles, a Web Audio BGM/SFX mixer (`./SoundEffects`), and a deterministic GitHub Actions cloud MP4 exporter (`16:9` widescreen & `9:16` vertical Shorts).

## Built-In POV Episodes
- **Grandpa Loses His Key & Granny's Mystery Box (44s, `16:9` POV)** — 3-skit first-person adventure: meet Grandpa in the living room, sneak past Granny and crouch-hide beside the bed, grab the glowing Golden Key from the kitchen, unlock the Mystery Chest, and survive Granny's rage chase!
- **Granny & Grandpa: Jump, Run, Walk (6s, `9:16` Short)** — quick jump, sprint, and stroll showcase.

## Studio Features
- **First-Person POV Camera (`src/playerCamera.js`)**: Eye-level A* indoor walking with head-bob, whip-pan double-takes (`player_look`), camera-head gestures (`nod_yes`, `shake_no`, `shake_panic`, `peek_lean`), crouch-hiding (`0.42m`), bottom-right FPS held-item viewmodel, fade-to-black, and `16:9` / `9:16` toggle.
- **Comedy Props & Thought Bubbles (`src/comedyProps.js`)**: `golden_key`, `padlock`, opening `treasure_chest`, `emoji_ball`, `toy_blaster`, `cookie_jar`, plus 8 comic thought bubbles (`key`, `question`, `exclamation`, `zzz`, `angry`, `laugh`, `food`, `heart`).
- **Web Audio BGM + 28-Clip Mixer (`src/sfxEngine.js`)**: Looping comedy & chase BGM (`SoundEffects/backroundMusics/`: `fluffing_a_duck`, `monkeys_spinning_monkeys`, `run_amok`, plus Granny ambience/chase tracks), `cutBgm` comedy silence cuts, movement-synced footsteps, whip-pan whooshes, bubble pops, and a toggleable 1–2s kids-comedy pacer.
- **Episode Production Guide (`EPISODE_PRODUCTION_GUIDE.md`)**: Complete playbook for generating high-retention Shorts & POV episodes (3-second pacing rules, doorway routing, BGM/SFX table, and copy-paste JSON template).
- **Waypoints & Live Skit Editor (`src/studioUI.js`)**: Click any indoor `SET_MAP` waypoint to teleport, scout new coordinates with `WASD + Q/E`, test animations/bubbles/SFX, or edit & play multi-skit JSON scripts live.

## Cloud Video Rendering (No Local Encoding Required)
Trigger **Actions → Export POV Episode (Cloud Render)** (`.github/workflows/export-video.yml`) or run:
```bash
gh workflow run export-video.yml --ref main -f preset=grandpa_key_pov_44s -f fps=30 -f sfx_pacer=true -f format=mp4
```
The rendered `.mp4` is automatically published to the `latest-export` GitHub Release.

## Run Locally
Start a static server from the repository root and open `http://localhost:8000`:
```bash
python3 -m http.server 8000
```

===== FILE: scripts/export-video.mjs =====
================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env node
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import http from "node:http";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..");

const MIME_TYPES = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".mjs": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".json": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
  ".webp": "image/webp",
  ".fbx": "application/octet-stream",
  ".mp3": "audio/mpeg",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
};

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const opts = {
    preset: "grandpa_key_pov_44s",
    episodeFile: "",
    aspect: "",
    fps: 30,
    pacer: true,
    format: "mp4",
    workers: 0,
    outDir: path.join(REPO_ROOT, "output"),
  };
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i += 1) {
    const a = argv[i];
    if (a === "--preset") opts.preset = argv[++i];
    else if (a === "--episode-file") opts.episodeFile = argv[++i];
    else if (a === "--aspect") opts.aspect = argv[++i] || "";
    else if (a === "--fps") opts.fps = Number(argv[++i]) || 30;
    else if (a === "--pacer") opts.pacer = argv[++i] !== "false";
    else if (a === "--format") opts.format = argv[++i] || "mp4";
    else if (a === "--workers") opts.workers = Number(argv[++i]) || 0;
    else if (a === "--out-dir") opts.outDir = path.resolve(argv[++i]);
  }
  return opts;
}

function startStaticServer(rootDir) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
      const reqPath = decodeURIComponent((req.url || "/").split("?")[0]);
      const rel = reqPath === "/" ? "index.html" : reqPath.replace(/^\/+/, "");
      const fullPath = path.resolve(rootDir, rel);

      if (!fullPath.startsWith(rootDir)) {
        res.writeHead(403);
        res.end("Forbidden");
        return;
      }

      if (!fs.existsSync(fullPath) || fs.statSync(fullPath).isDirectory()) {
        res.writeHead(404);
        res.end("Not found");
        return;
      }

      const ext = path.extname(fullPath).toLowerCase();
      res.writeHead(200, {
        "Content-Type": MIME_TYPES[ext] || "application/octet-stream",
        "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
      });
      fs.createReadStream(fullPath).pipe(res);
    });
    server.on("error", reject);
    server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", () => {
      const addr = server.address();
      resolve({ server, port: addr.port });
    });
  });
}

function runFfmpeg(args) {
  const res = spawnSync("ffmpeg", args, { stdio: "inherit" });
  if (res.status !== 0) {
    throw new Error(`ffmpeg exited with code ${res.status}`);
  }
}

async function openStudioSession({
  chromium,
  port,
  localThreeDir,
  hasLocalThree,
  episode,
  pacer,
  aspect,
}) {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({
    headless: true,
    args: [
      "--use-gl=angle",
      "--use-angle=swiftshader",
      "--enable-webgl",
      "--ignore-gpu-blocklist",
      "--no-sandbox",
      "--disable-dev-shm-usage",
      "--disable-background-timer-throttling",
      "--disable-renderer-backgrounding",
    ],
  });

  const context = await browser.newContext({
    viewport: { width: 1280, height: 1280 },
    deviceScaleFactor: 1,
  });
  const page = await context.newPage();

  const errors = [];
  page.on("pageerror", (err) => errors.push(`pageerror: ${err.message}`));
  page.on("console", (msg) => {
    if (msg.type() === "error") {
      errors.push(`console.error: ${msg.text()}`);
    }
  });

  if (hasLocalThree) {
    for (const prefix of [
      "https://unpkg.com/three@0.170.0/",
      "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.170.0/",
    ]) {
      await page.route(`${prefix}**`, async (route) => {
        const url = route.request().url();
        const sub = url.replace(prefix, "");
        const localFile = path.resolve(localThreeDir, sub);
        if (localFile.startsWith(localThreeDir) && fs.existsSync(localFile)) {
          await route.fulfill({
            status: 200,
            contentType: "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
            body: fs.readFileSync(localFile),
          });
          return;
        }
        await route.continue();
      });
    }
  }

  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/?headless=1`, {
    waitUntil: "domcontentloaded",
    timeout: 120000,
  });

  await page.waitForFunction(
    () => Boolean(window.__GRANNY_STUDIO__?.ready || window.__GRANNY_STUDIO_ERROR__),
    { timeout: 120000 }
  );

  const startupErr = await page.evaluate(() => window.__GRANNY_STUDIO_ERROR__ || null);
  if (startupErr) {
    await browser.close();
    throw new Error(`Studio startup failed: ${startupErr}`);
  }

  const meta = await page.evaluate(
    ({ ep, pacerEnabled, aspectOverride }) =>
      window.__GRANNY_STUDIO__.beginHeadlessExport({
        episode: ep,
        pacerEnabled,
        aspectOverride,
      }),
    { ep: episode, pacerEnabled: pacer, aspectOverride: aspect }
  );

  return { browser, page, errors, meta };
}

function buildSfxFilterChain(ev, inIdx, outLbl) {
  const delayMs = Math.max(0, Math.round(ev.time * 1000));
  const pitch = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2.0, Number(ev.pitch) || 1.0));
  const vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(2.0, Number(ev.volume) || 0.7));
  const chain = [
    `[${inIdx}:a]aformat=sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo`,
  ];
  if (ev.maxDuration && ev.maxDuration > 0) {
    chain.push(`atrim=0:${ev.maxDuration}`);
  }
  if (Math.abs(pitch - 1.0) > 0.005) {
    chain.push(`asetrate=44100*${pitch.toFixed(4)},aresample=44100`);
  }
  chain.push(`volume=${vol.toFixed(3)}`);
  if (delayMs > 0) {
    chain.push(`adelay=${delayMs}|${delayMs}`);
  }
  return `${chain.join(",")}${outLbl}`;
}

function preMixSfxBatches(sfxEvents, duration, framesDir, batchSize = 40) {
  const validEvents = sfxEvents.filter((ev) =>
    fs.existsSync(path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, ev.path))
  );
  if (validEvents.length <= batchSize) {
    return { batchWavPaths: [], remainingEvents: validEvents };
  }

  const batchWavPaths = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < validEvents.length; i += batchSize) {
    const chunk = validEvents.slice(i, i + batchSize);
    const batchIdx = Math.floor(i / batchSize);
    const wavPath = path.join(framesDir, `sfx_batch_${batchIdx}.wav`);

    const inputs = [
      "-y",
      "-f",
      "lavfi",
      "-t",
      String(duration),
      "-i",
      "anullsrc=channel_layout=stereo:sample_rate=44100",
    ];
    const filters = ["[0:a]aformat=sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo[a0]"];
    const labels = ["[a0]"];

    chunk.forEach((ev, idx) => {
      const inIdx = idx + 1;
      inputs.push("-i", path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, ev.path));
      const lbl = `[s${inIdx}]`;
      filters.push(buildSfxFilterChain(ev, inIdx, lbl));
      labels.push(lbl);
    });

    filters.push(
      `${labels.join("")}amix=inputs=${labels.length}:duration=first:dropout_transition=0:normalize=0[bout]`
    );

    runFfmpeg([
      ...inputs,
      "-filter_complex",
      filters.join(";"),
      "-map",
      "[bout]",
      "-t",
      String(duration),
      "-c:a",
      "pcm_f32le",
      wavPath,
    ]);
    batchWavPaths.push(wavPath);
  }
  console.log(
    `[Export] Pre-mixed ${validEvents.length} SFX events into ${batchWavPaths.length} intermediate stems.`
  );
  return { batchWavPaths, remainingEvents: [] };
}

async function main() {
  const opts = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  const { chromium } = await import("playwright");
  const { EPISODE_PRESETS } = await import(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "src/config.js"));

  let episode = null;
  const envJson = (process.env.EPISODE_JSON || "").trim();
  if (opts.episodeFile) {
    episode = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(opts.episodeFile), "utf8"));
  } else if (envJson) {
    episode = JSON.parse(envJson);
  } else if (opts.preset && EPISODE_PRESETS[opts.preset]) {
    episode = EPISODE_PRESETS[opts.preset];
  } else {
    throw new Error(
      `Unknown preset "${opts.preset}". Available: ${Object.keys(EPISODE_PRESETS).join(", ")}`
    );
  }

  fs.mkdirSync(opts.outDir, { recursive: true });
  const framesDir = path.join("/tmp", `granny-frames-${Date.now()}`);
  fs.mkdirSync(framesDir, { recursive: true });

  const { server, port } = await startStaticServer(REPO_ROOT);
  const localThreeDir = path.join(REPO_ROOT, "node_modules/three");
  const hasLocalThree = fs.existsSync(localThreeDir);

  const activeBrowsers = [];

  try {
    console.log(`[Export] Launching primary studio on http://127.0.0.1:${port}/?headless=1`);
    const primary = await openStudioSession({
      chromium,
      port,
      localThreeDir,
      hasLocalThree,
      episode,
      pacer: opts.pacer,
      aspect: opts.aspect,
    });
    activeBrowsers.push(primary.browser);

    const meta = primary.meta;
    const fps = opts.fps;
    const totalFrames = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(meta.duration * fps));
    const stepDt = 1 / fps;

    const autoWorkers = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, os.cpus().length));
    let workerCount = opts.workers > 0 ? opts.workers : autoWorkers;
    if (totalFrames < 120) workerCount = 1;
    workerCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(workerCount, totalFrames));

    console.log(
      `[Export] Rendering "${meta.title}" [${meta.aspectKey} ${meta.width}x${meta.height}] (${meta.duration.toFixed(2)}s @ ${fps}fps = ${totalFrames} frames) across ${workerCount} worker(s)...`
    );

    const renderStartMs = Date.now();
    const ranges = [];
    for (let w = 0; w < workerCount; w += 1) {
      const startIdx = Math.floor((w * totalFrames) / workerCount);
      const endIdx = Math.floor(((w + 1) * totalFrames) / workerCount);
      ranges.push({ workerId: w, startIdx, endIdx });
    }

    async function runWorker(range, existingSession = null) {
      const session =
        existingSession ||
        (await openStudioSession({
          chromium,
          port,
          localThreeDir,
          hasLocalThree,
          episode,
          pacer: opts.pacer,
          aspect: opts.aspect,
        }));
      if (!existingSession) {
        activeBrowsers.push(session.browser);
      }

      const { page, errors } = session;
      const { workerId, startIdx, endIdx } = range;

      if (startIdx > 0) {
        await page.evaluate(
          ({ count, dt }) => window.__GRANNY_STUDIO__.advanceExportFrames(count, dt),
          { count: startIdx - 1, dt: stepDt }
        );
      }

      for (let idx = startIdx; idx < endIdx; idx += 1) {
        const frame = await page.evaluate(
          (dt) => window.__GRANNY_STUDIO__.renderExportFrame(dt),
          idx === 0 ? 0 : stepDt
        );
        const base64 = frame.dataUrl.split(",")[1];
        const ext = frame.dataUrl.startsWith("data:image/jpeg") ? "jpg" : "png";
        const framePath = path.join(framesDir, `frame_${String(idx).padStart(5, "0")}.${ext}`);
        fs.writeFileSync(framePath, Buffer.from(base64, "base64"));

        const doneInChunk = idx - startIdx + 1;
        const chunkLen = endIdx - startIdx;
        if (doneInChunk % 60 === 0 || idx === endIdx - 1) {
          const elapsedS = ((Date.now() - renderStartMs) / 1000).toFixed(1);
          console.log(
            `[Worker ${workerId}] ${doneInChunk}/${chunkLen} (global frame ${idx + 1}/${totalFrames}, t=${frame.simTime.toFixed(2)}s, elapsed=${elapsedS}s)`
          );
        }
      }

      let audioCapture = null;
      if (workerId === 0) {
        if (endIdx < totalFrames) {
          await page.evaluate(
            ({ count, dt }) => window.__GRANNY_STUDIO__.advanceExportFrames(count, dt),
            { count: totalFrames - endIdx, dt: stepDt }
          );
        }
        audioCapture = await page.evaluate(() =>
          window.__GRANNY_STUDIO__.finishHeadlessExport()
        );
      }

      if (errors.length > 0) {
        throw new Error(`Browser errors in worker ${workerId}:\n${errors.join("\n")}`);
      }

      if (!existingSession) {
        await session.browser.close();
      }
      return audioCapture;
    }

    const workerResults = await Promise.all(
      ranges.map((r) => runWorker(r, r.workerId === 0 ? primary : null))
    );

    const audioCapture = workerResults[0];
    await primary.browser.close();

    const frameExt = fs.existsSync(path.join(framesDir, "frame_00000.jpg")) ? "jpg" : "png";
    const sfxEvents = audioCapture?.sfx || [];
    const bgmSegments = audioCapture?.bgm || [];
    const bgmDucks = audioCapture?.ducks || [];

    console.log(
      `[Export] All ${totalFrames} frames rendered in ${((Date.now() - renderStartMs) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s. Captured ${sfxEvents.length} SFX events, ${bgmSegments.length} BGM segments, ${bgmDucks.length} duck cuts.`
    );

    const slug = (meta.title || "granny-pov-episode")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
      .replace(/^-|-$/g, "");

    const ffInputs = [
      "-y",
      "-framerate",
      String(fps),
      "-i",
      path.join(framesDir, `frame_%05d.${frameExt}`),
      "-f",
      "lavfi",
      "-t",
      String(meta.duration),
      "-i",
      "anullsrc=channel_layout=stereo:sample_rate=44100",
    ];

    const filterParts = ["[1:a]aformat=sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo[a0]"];
    const mixLabels = ["[a0]"];
    let nextInputIdx = 2;

    // 1. Mix looping BGM segments with cutBgm ducking windows
    const duckExpr =
      bgmDucks.length > 0
        ? bgmDucks
            .map((d) => `between(t,${d.startTime.toFixed(3)},${d.endTime.toFixed(3)})`)
            .join("+")
        : "";

    for (const seg of bgmSegments) {
      const bgmFullPath = path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, seg.path);
      if (!fs.existsSync(bgmFullPath)) continue;
      const segDur = Math.max(0.05, (seg.endTime ?? meta.duration) - seg.startTime);
      const delayMs = Math.max(0, Math.round(seg.startTime * 1000));
      const vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(1.5, Number(seg.volume) || 0.24));

      ffInputs.push("-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", segDur.toFixed(3), "-i", bgmFullPath);
      const inIdx = nextInputIdx++;
      const outLbl = `[bgm${inIdx}]`;

      const chain = [
        `[${inIdx}:a]aformat=sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo`,
        `volume=${vol.toFixed(3)}`,
      ];
      if (delayMs > 0) {
        chain.push(`adelay=${delayMs}|${delayMs}`);
      }
      if (duckExpr) {
        chain.push(`volume=enable='${duckExpr}':volume=0`);
      }
      filterParts.push(`${chain.join(",")}${outLbl}`);
      mixLabels.push(outLbl);
    }

    // 2. Mix SFX clips (batched into intermediate stems when > 40 events)
    const { batchWavPaths, remainingEvents } = preMixSfxBatches(
      sfxEvents,
      meta.duration,
      framesDir,
      40
    );

    for (const wavPath of batchWavPaths) {
      const inIdx = nextInputIdx++;
      ffInputs.push("-i", wavPath);
      const outLbl = `[stem${inIdx}]`;
      filterParts.push(`[${inIdx}:a]anull${outLbl}`);
      mixLabels.push(outLbl);
    }

    for (const ev of remainingEvents) {
      const sfxFullPath = path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, ev.path);
      if (!fs.existsSync(sfxFullPath)) continue;
      const inIdx = nextInputIdx++;
      ffInputs.push("-i", sfxFullPath);
      const outLbl = `[sfx${inIdx}]`;
      filterParts.push(buildSfxFilterChain(ev, inIdx, outLbl));
      mixLabels.push(outLbl);
    }

    filterParts.push(
      `${mixLabels.join("")}amix=inputs=${mixLabels.length}:duration=first:dropout_transition=0:normalize=0,` +
        `acompressor=threshold=-10dB:ratio=4:attack=5:release=120,alimiter=limit=0.95[aout]`
    );

    const formats = opts.format === "both" ? ["mp4", "webm"] : [opts.format];
    const producedFiles = [];

    for (const fmt of formats) {
      const outPath = path.join(opts.outDir, `${slug}.${fmt}`);
      const codecArgs =
        fmt === "webm"
          ? ["-c:v", "libvpx-vp9", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-crf", "24", "-b:v", "0", "-c:a", "libopus", "-b:a", "128k"]
          : ["-c:v", "libx264", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-preset", "medium", "-crf", "18", "-movflags", "+faststart", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k"];

      runFfmpeg([
        ...ffInputs,
        "-filter_complex",
        filterParts.join(";"),
        "-map",
        "0:v:0",
        "-map",
        "[aout]",
        "-t",
        String(meta.duration),
        ...codecArgs,
        outPath,
      ]);

      const sizeMb = (fs.statSync(outPath).size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2);
      producedFiles.push({ path: outPath, file: path.basename(outPath), sizeMb });
      console.log(`[Export] Wrote ${outPath} (${sizeMb} MB)`);
    }

    fs.writeFileSync(
      path.join(opts.outDir, "export-summary.json"),
      JSON.stringify(
        {
          title: meta.title,
          duration: meta.duration,
          aspectKey: meta.aspectKey,
          width: meta.width,
          height: meta.height,
          fps,
          totalFrames,
          sfxCount: sfxEvents.length,
          bgmCount: bgmSegments.length,
          files: producedFiles,
        },
        null,
        2
      )
    );
  } finally {
    for (const b of activeBrowsers) {
      try {
        await b.close();
      } catch {
        // ignore already-closed browser
      }
    }
    server.close();
    fs.rmSync(framesDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});
