import * as THREE from "three";

const BUBBLE_ICONS = {
  key: { symbol: "KEY", bg: "#fef08a", accent: "#ca8a04", draw: "key" },
  question: { symbol: "?", bg: "#e0f2fe", accent: "#0284c7", draw: "text" },
  exclamation: { symbol: "!", bg: "#fee2e2", accent: "#dc2626", draw: "text" },
  zzz: { symbol: "ZZZ", bg: "#ede9fe", accent: "#6d28d9", draw: "text" },
  angry: { symbol: "GRR!", bg: "#fecaca", accent: "#b91c1c", draw: "angry" },
  laugh: { symbol: "HAHA", bg: "#fef9c3", accent: "#ea580c", draw: "laugh" },
  food: { symbol: "COOKIE", bg: "#ffedd5", accent: "#c2410c", draw: "cookie" },
  heart: { symbol: "LOVE", bg: "#fce7f3", accent: "#db2777", draw: "heart" },
};

function drawComicBubbleTexture(iconName) {
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = 256;
  canvas.height = 192;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!ctx) return null;

  const spec = BUBBLE_ICONS[iconName] || BUBBLE_ICONS.question;

  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 256, 192);

  // Comic cloud / speech bubble body
  ctx.fillStyle = spec.bg;
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#0f172a";
  ctx.lineWidth = 10;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(22, 16, 212, 128, 42);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.stroke();

  // Thought tail dots
  for (const [cx, cy, r] of [
    [118, 158, 12],
    [104, 178, 7],
  ]) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = 6;
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(128, 80);

  if (spec.draw === "key") {
    // Bold golden key icon
    ctx.fillStyle = "#facc15";
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#78350f";
    ctx.lineWidth = 7;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(-44, 0, 24, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = spec.bg;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(-44, 0, 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = "#facc15";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.roundRect(-20, -8, 86, 16, 5);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillRect(34, 8, 12, 18);
    ctx.strokeRect(34, 8, 12, 18);
    ctx.fillRect(52, 8, 12, 14);
    ctx.strokeRect(52, 8, 12, 14);
  } else if (spec.draw === "angry") {
    // Comic anger vein + furious eyes
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#dc2626";
    ctx.lineWidth = 10;
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-42, -22);
    ctx.lineTo(-10, -4);
    ctx.moveTo(42, -22);
    ctx.lineTo(10, -4);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f172a";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(-26, 10, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.arc(26, 10, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  } else if (spec.draw === "laugh") {
    // Bold icon-first laughing emoji face (no small text)
    ctx.fillStyle = "#facc15";
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#854d0e";
    ctx.lineWidth = 7;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, 0, 42, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();
    // Happy > < eyes
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#0f172a";
    ctx.lineWidth = 6;
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-24, -18);
    ctx.lineTo(-10, -10);
    ctx.lineTo(-24, -2);
    ctx.moveTo(24, -18);
    ctx.lineTo(10, -10);
    ctx.lineTo(24, -2);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Big open laughing mouth
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7f1d1d";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, 6, 22, 0, Math.PI);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = "#fb7185";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, 21, 11, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  } else if (spec.draw === "cookie") {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#d97706";
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#78350f";
    ctx.lineWidth = 7;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, 0, 38, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = "#451a03";
    for (const [cx, cy] of [
      [-14, -12],
      [14, -10],
      [-6, 14],
      [16, 12],
      [0, -2],
    ]) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  } else if (spec.draw === "heart") {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ec4899";
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#831843";
    ctx.lineWidth = 6;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, 28);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(-48, -4, -34, -40, 0, -14);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(34, -40, 48, -4, 0, 28);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();
  } else {
    ctx.fillStyle = spec.accent;
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#0f172a";
    ctx.lineWidth = 8;
    ctx.font = "900 72px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.strokeText(spec.symbol, 0, 4);
    ctx.fillText(spec.symbol, 0, 4);
  }

  ctx.restore();

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

/**
 * Manages cached comic thought-bubble textures and per-character floating sprites.
 */
export class ThoughtBubbleCache {
  constructor() {
    this.textures = new Map();
  }

  getTexture(iconName) {
    if (!this.textures.has(iconName)) {
      const tex = drawComicBubbleTexture(iconName);
      if (tex) this.textures.set(iconName, tex);
    }
    return this.textures.get(iconName) || null;
  }

  dispose() {
    for (const tex of this.textures.values()) {
      tex.dispose();
    }
    this.textures.clear();
  }
}

function makeLavaTexture() {
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = 512;
  canvas.height = 512;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!ctx) return null;

  ctx.fillStyle = "#b91c1c";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 512);

  const blobs = [
    [90, 80, 68, "#f97316"], [240, 120, 85, "#fb923c"], [410, 90, 72, "#facc15"],
    [150, 260, 92, "#ea580c"], [340, 280, 76, "#facc15"], [440, 400, 82, "#f97316"],
    [80, 410, 74, "#fb923c"], [250, 430, 88, "#ea580c"], [40, 220, 54, "#fef08a"],
    [300, 50, 50, "#fef08a"], [460, 240, 60, "#fef08a"], [190, 180, 42, "#450a0a"],
    [360, 170, 38, "#7c2d12"], [120, 340, 44, "#450a0a"], [330, 380, 46, "#7c2d12"],
  ];
  for (const [cx, cy, r, col] of blobs) {
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.repeat.set(9, 9);
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

function makePackageLabelTexture() {
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = 256;
  canvas.height = 128;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!ctx) return null;

  ctx.fillStyle = "#fef3c7";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 128);
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#dc2626";
  ctx.lineWidth = 8;
  ctx.strokeRect(8, 8, 240, 112);
  ctx.fillStyle = "#dc2626";
  ctx.font = "900 34px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
  ctx.fillText("MYSTERY!", 128, 48);
  ctx.fillStyle = "#0f172a";
  ctx.font = "800 22px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText("DO NOT PRESS", 128, 92);

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

function makeEmojiFaceTexture(angry = false) {
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = 512;
  canvas.height = 256;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!ctx) return null;

  ctx.fillStyle = angry ? "#fb923c" : "#facc15";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 256);

  const drawFaceAt = (cx, cy) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f172a";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx - 48, cy - 22, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.arc(cx + 48, cy - 22, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    if (angry) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#7f1d1d";
      ctx.lineWidth = 12;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(cx - 70, cy - 52);
      ctx.lineTo(cx - 22, cy - 34);
      ctx.moveTo(cx + 70, cy - 52);
      ctx.lineTo(cx + 22, cy - 34);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy + 48, 36, Math.PI * 1.15, Math.PI * 1.85);
      ctx.stroke();
    } else {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx - 52, cy - 26, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.arc(cx + 44, cy - 26, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      ctx.strokeStyle = "#0f172a";
      ctx.lineWidth = 12;
      ctx.lineCap = "round";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy + 12, 48, 0.15 * Math.PI, 0.85 * Math.PI);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  };

  // Mirror emoji face on BOTH front (u=0.5) and back (u=0/1 seam) hemispheres
  drawFaceAt(256, 128);
  drawFaceAt(0, 128);
  drawFaceAt(512, 128);

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

/**
 * Builds reusable low-poly cartoon slapstick props (Golden Key, Padlock,
 * Opening Treasure Chest, Emoji Ball, Toy Blaster, Cookie Jar).
 */
export function createPropMesh(propType) {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.name = `Prop_${propType}`;

  switch (propType) {
    case "golden_key": {
      const gold = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xfacc15,
        emissive: 0xca8a04,
        emissiveIntensity: 0.35,
        roughness: 0.25,
        metalness: 0.75,
      });
      const bow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.085, 0.022, 10, 20), gold);
      bow.position.set(0, 0.09, 0);
      const shaft = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.018, 0.018, 0.24, 10), gold);
      shaft.position.set(0, -0.07, 0);
      const tooth1 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.055, 0.028, 0.02), gold);
      tooth1.position.set(0.035, -0.14, 0);
      const tooth2 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.045, 0.025, 0.02), gold);
      tooth2.position.set(0.03, -0.18, 0);
      group.add(bow, shaft, tooth1, tooth2);
      group.rotation.z = -0.35;
      break;
    }

    case "padlock": {
      const brass = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xf59e0b,
        roughness: 0.32,
        metalness: 0.65,
      });
      const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xe2e8f0,
        roughness: 0.28,
        metalness: 0.8,
      });
      const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.18, 0.16, 0.08), brass);
      body.position.y = 0.08;
      const shackle = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.06, 0.016, 10, 18, Math.PI),
        steel
      );
      shackle.position.y = 0.16;
      shackle.name = "Shackle";
      group.add(body, shackle);
      break;
    }

    case "treasure_chest": {
      const wood = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x92400e, roughness: 0.65 });
      const gold = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xfbbf24,
        roughness: 0.3,
        metalness: 0.7,
      });
      const glow = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xfef08a,
        emissive: 0xfacc15,
        emissiveIntensity: 0.9,
      });

      const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.72, 0.36, 0.48), wood);
      base.position.y = 0.18;
      base.castShadow = true;
      base.receiveShadow = true;

      const bandL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.06, 0.38, 0.5), gold);
      bandL.position.set(-0.24, 0.19, 0);
      const bandR = bandL.clone();
      bandR.position.x = 0.24;

      const innerGlow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.6, 0.08, 0.36), glow);
      innerGlow.position.y = 0.36;
      innerGlow.visible = false;
      innerGlow.name = "ChestGlow";

      // Golden confetti/sparkle burst group inside chest
      const sparkles = new THREE.Group();
      sparkles.name = "ChestSparkles";
      sparkles.visible = false;
      const sparkColors = [0xfef08a, 0x38bdf8, 0xf472b6, 0x4ade80];
      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i += 1) {
        const sMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
          color: sparkColors[i % sparkColors.length],
        });
        const sMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.055, 0), sMat);
        const angle = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
        sMesh.userData = {
          vx: Math.cos(angle) * 0.65,
          vy: 1.5 + (i % 3) * 0.35,
          vz: Math.sin(angle) * 0.65,
        };
        sMesh.position.set(0, 0.36, 0);
        sparkles.add(sMesh);
      }

      const lidPivot = new THREE.Group();
      lidPivot.name = "ChestLid";
      lidPivot.position.set(0, 0.36, -0.24);
      const lid = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.74, 0.18, 0.5), wood);
      lid.position.set(0, 0.09, 0.24);
      lid.castShadow = true;
      const lidTrim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.76, 0.05, 0.52), gold);
      lidTrim.position.set(0, 0.02, 0.24);
      const lockPlate = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.11, 0.12, 0.04), gold);
      lockPlate.position.set(0, 0.0, 0.49);
      lidPivot.add(lid, lidTrim, lockPlate);

      group.add(base, bandL, bandR, innerGlow, sparkles, lidPivot);
      break;
    }

    case "emoji_ball": {
      const tex = makeEmojiFaceTexture(false);
      const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        map: tex,
        color: 0xffffff,
        roughness: 0.3,
      });
      const sphere = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.28, 24, 18), mat);
      sphere.position.y = 0.28;
      sphere.rotation.y = -Math.PI * 0.5;
      sphere.castShadow = true;
      group.add(sphere);
      break;
    }

    case "toy_blaster": {
      const bodyMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xf97316, roughness: 0.4 });
      const accentMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x38bdf8, roughness: 0.35 });
      const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.09, 0.34), bodyMat);
      barrel.position.set(0, 0.07, 0);
      const grip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.06, 0.14, 0.07), accentMat);
      grip.position.set(0, -0.02, 0.09);
      grip.rotation.x = 0.25;
      const muzzle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.036, 0.036, 0.06, 12), accentMat);
      muzzle.rotation.x = Math.PI * 0.5;
      muzzle.position.set(0, 0.07, -0.18);
      group.add(barrel, grip, muzzle);
      break;
    }

    case "delivery_box": {
      const cardboard = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc28e5c, roughness: 0.78 });
      const tape = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xfde68a, roughness: 0.45 });
      const labelMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: makePackageLabelTexture() });

      const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.62, 0.42, 0.52), cardboard);
      body.position.y = 0.21;
      body.castShadow = true;
      body.receiveShadow = true;

      const labelFront = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.42, 0.22), labelMat);
      labelFront.position.set(0, 0.22, -0.265);
      labelFront.rotation.y = Math.PI;
      const labelBack = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.42, 0.22), labelMat);
      labelBack.position.set(0, 0.22, 0.265);

      const flapLPivot = new THREE.Group();
      flapLPivot.name = "BoxFlapL";
      flapLPivot.position.set(-0.31, 0.42, 0);
      const flapL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.31, 0.03, 0.52), cardboard);
      flapL.position.set(0.155, 0.015, 0);
      const tapeL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.31, 0.034, 0.1), tape);
      tapeL.position.set(0.155, 0.016, 0);
      flapLPivot.add(flapL, tapeL);

      const flapRPivot = new THREE.Group();
      flapRPivot.name = "BoxFlapR";
      flapRPivot.position.set(0.31, 0.42, 0);
      const flapR = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.31, 0.03, 0.52), cardboard);
      flapR.position.set(-0.155, 0.015, 0);
      const tapeR = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.31, 0.034, 0.1), tape);
      tapeR.position.set(-0.155, 0.016, 0);
      flapRPivot.add(flapR, tapeR);

      group.add(body, labelFront, labelBack, flapLPivot, flapRPivot);
      break;
    }

    case "red_button": {
      const baseMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1e293b, roughness: 0.4, metalness: 0.5 });
      const warnMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xfacc15, roughness: 0.35 });
      const btnMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xef4444,
        emissive: 0xdc2626,
        emissiveIntensity: 0.55,
        roughness: 0.25,
      });

      const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.42, 0.09, 0.42), baseMat);
      base.position.y = 0.045;
      base.castShadow = true;
      const trim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.45, 0.035, 0.45), warnMat);
      trim.position.y = 0.025;
      const collar = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.16, 0.18, 0.05, 18), baseMat);
      collar.position.y = 0.11;

      const cap = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.13, 0.145, 0.09, 20), btnMat);
      cap.position.y = 0.155;
      cap.name = "ButtonCap";
      cap.castShadow = true;

      group.add(base, trim, collar, cap);
      break;
    }

    case "rising_lava": {
      const lavaTex = makeLavaTexture();
      const lavaMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        map: lavaTex,
        color: 0xffaa44,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.92,
      });
      const plane = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(44, 44, 1, 1), lavaMat);
      plane.rotation.x = -Math.PI * 0.5;
      plane.name = "LavaPlane";
      group.add(plane);
      break;
    }

    case "cookie_jar":
    default: {
      const jarMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x38bdf8, roughness: 0.3 });
      const lidMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xfef08a, roughness: 0.35 });
      const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.16, 0.18, 0.32, 16), jarMat);
      body.position.y = 0.16;
      const lid = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.14, 0.16, 0.06, 16), lidMat);
      lid.position.y = 0.34;
      const knob = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.045, 12, 10), lidMat);
      knob.position.y = 0.39;
      group.add(body, lid, knob);
      break;
    }
  }

  return group;
}

/**
 * Manages placed gag props in the scene and drives animations (open, close, bounce, spin, pop_out, hide).
 */
export class ComedyPropManager {
  constructor(scene, waypoints) {
    this.scene = scene;
    this.waypoints = waypoints;
    this.group = new THREE.Group();
    this.group.name = "ComedyProps";
    this.scene.add(this.group);

    this.props = new Map(); // id -> { id, type, mesh, basePos, state }
  }

  loadProps(propDefs = []) {
    this.clear();
    for (const def of propDefs) {
      const mesh = createPropMesh(def.type);
      const wp = this.waypoints[def.spawn];
      const basePos = wp
        ? new THREE.Vector3(wp.x, wp.y + (def.yOffset || 0), wp.z)
        : new THREE.Vector3(0, def.yOffset || 0, 0);

      mesh.position.copy(basePos);
      if (def.rotationY) mesh.rotation.y = def.rotationY;
      if (def.scale) mesh.scale.setScalar(def.scale);
      mesh.visible = def.visible !== false;

      this.group.add(mesh);
      this.props.set(def.id, {
        id: def.id,
        type: def.type,
        mesh,
        basePos: basePos.clone(),
        state: {
          spin: Boolean(def.spin),
          float: Boolean(def.float),
          bounce: false,
          popOut: false,
          lidOpen: false,
          pressed: false,
          riseActive: false,
          riseStartY: basePos.y,
          riseTargetY: basePos.y,
          riseElapsed: 0,
          riseDuration: 1,
          phase: 0,
        },
      });
    }
  }

  getProp(id) {
    return this.props.get(id) || null;
  }

  performAction(propId, action, params = {}) {
    const entry = this.props.get(propId);
    if (!entry) return;
    const { mesh, state, basePos } = entry;

    switch (action) {
      case "show":
        mesh.visible = true;
        state.bounce = false;
        state.popOut = false;
        state.spin = false;
        if (params.at && this.waypoints[params.at]) {
          const wp = this.waypoints[params.at];
          basePos.set(wp.x, wp.y + (params.yOffset || 0), wp.z);
          mesh.position.copy(basePos);
        }
        break;
      case "hide":
      case "pickup":
        mesh.visible = false;
        state.bounce = false;
        state.popOut = false;
        state.spin = false;
        break;
      case "open":
        mesh.visible = true;
        state.lidOpen = true;
        state.sparklePhase = 0;
        {
          const glow = mesh.getObjectByName("ChestGlow");
          if (glow) glow.visible = true;
          const sparkles = mesh.getObjectByName("ChestSparkles");
          if (sparkles) {
            sparkles.visible = true;
            for (const c of sparkles.children) {
              c.position.set(0, 0.36, 0);
              c.scale.setScalar(1);
            }
          }
        }
        break;
      case "press":
        mesh.visible = true;
        state.pressed = true;
        break;
      case "unpress":
        state.pressed = false;
        break;
      case "rise_to":
        mesh.visible = true;
        state.riseActive = true;
        state.riseStartY = basePos.y;
        state.riseTargetY = params.targetY ?? basePos.y + 0.5;
        state.riseElapsed = 0;
        state.riseDuration = Math.max(0.1, params.duration || 3.0);
        break;
      case "close":
        state.lidOpen = false;
        {
          const glow = mesh.getObjectByName("ChestGlow");
          if (glow) glow.visible = false;
          const sparkles = mesh.getObjectByName("ChestSparkles");
          if (sparkles) sparkles.visible = false;
        }
        break;
      case "pop_out":
        mesh.visible = true;
        state.popOut = true;
        state.bounce = true;
        state.spin = entry.type !== "emoji_ball";
        state.phase = 0;
        if (params.at && this.waypoints[params.at]) {
          const wp = this.waypoints[params.at];
          basePos.set(wp.x, wp.y + (params.yOffset || 0), wp.z);
          mesh.position.copy(basePos);
        }
        state.popStartPos = basePos.clone();
        if (params.to && this.waypoints[params.to]) {
          const dst = this.waypoints[params.to];
          state.popEndPos = new THREE.Vector3(dst.x, dst.y + 0.02, dst.z);
          state.popTravelDur = Math.max(0.6, params.travelDuration || 2.2);
        } else {
          state.popEndPos = null;
        }
        break;
      case "bounce":
        mesh.visible = true;
        state.bounce = true;
        break;
      case "stop":
        state.bounce = false;
        state.spin = false;
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
  }

  update(dt, cameraPos = null) {
    for (const entry of this.props.values()) {
      const { mesh, state, basePos } = entry;
      if (!mesh.visible) continue;

      state.phase += dt;

      if (entry.type === "emoji_ball") {
        // Yaw-track camera with playful cartoon bounce wobble so the emoji face stays visible
        if (cameraPos) {
          const dx = cameraPos.x - mesh.position.x;
          const dz = cameraPos.z - mesh.position.z;
          if (dx * dx + dz * dz > 1e-4) {
            mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
          }
        }
        mesh.rotation.z = Math.sin(state.phase * 7.0) * 0.22;
        mesh.rotation.x = Math.cos(state.phase * 5.5) * 0.12;
      } else if (state.spin) {
        mesh.rotation.y += dt * 3.4;
      }

      if (state.popOut && state.popEndPos) {
        const u = Math.min(1, state.phase / state.popTravelDur);
        basePos.lerpVectors(state.popStartPos, state.popEndPos, u);
        mesh.position.x = basePos.x;
        mesh.position.z = basePos.z;
      }

      if (state.riseActive) {
        state.riseElapsed = Math.min(state.riseDuration, state.riseElapsed + dt);
        const rt = state.riseElapsed / state.riseDuration;
        basePos.y = state.riseStartY + (state.riseTargetY - state.riseStartY) * rt;
        mesh.position.y = basePos.y + Math.sin(state.phase * 3.5) * 0.025;
        if (rt >= 1) state.riseActive = false;
      } else if (entry.type === "rising_lava") {
        mesh.position.y = basePos.y + Math.sin(state.phase * 3.5) * 0.025;
      }

      if (entry.type === "rising_lava") {
        const lavaPlane = mesh.getObjectByName("LavaPlane");
        if (lavaPlane?.material?.map) {
          lavaPlane.material.map.offset.x = (state.phase * 0.08) % 1;
          lavaPlane.material.map.offset.y = (state.phase * 0.05) % 1;
        }
      }

      if (state.float) {
        mesh.position.y = basePos.y + Math.sin(state.phase * 3.5) * 0.06;
      } else if (state.bounce) {
        // Initial dramatic +0.95m spring launch out of chest, then lively bounce
        const launch =
          state.popOut && state.phase < 0.75
            ? Math.sin((state.phase / 0.75) * Math.PI) * 0.95
            : 0;
        const hop = Math.abs(Math.sin(state.phase * 6.0)) * 0.58;
        mesh.position.y = basePos.y + Math.max(launch, hop);
      }

      const lid = mesh.getObjectByName("ChestLid");
      if (lid) {
        const targetAngle = state.lidOpen ? -1.95 : 0;
        lid.rotation.x += (targetAngle - lid.rotation.x) * Math.min(1, dt * 12);
      }

      const flapL = mesh.getObjectByName("BoxFlapL");
      const flapR = mesh.getObjectByName("BoxFlapR");
      if (flapL && flapR) {
        const openAngle = state.lidOpen ? 2.15 : 0;
        flapL.rotation.z += (openAngle - flapL.rotation.z) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);
        flapR.rotation.z += (-openAngle - flapR.rotation.z) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);
      }

      const btnCap = mesh.getObjectByName("ButtonCap");
      if (btnCap) {
        const targetCapY = state.pressed ? 0.118 : 0.155;
        btnCap.position.y += (targetCapY - btnCap.position.y) * Math.min(1, dt * 18);
        if (btnCap.material) {
          btnCap.material.emissiveIntensity = state.pressed
            ? 1.15
            : 0.45 + Math.sin(state.phase * 6) * 0.25;
        }
      }

      const sparkles = mesh.getObjectByName("ChestSparkles");
      if (sparkles && sparkles.visible) {
        state.sparklePhase = (state.sparklePhase || 0) + dt;
        const spT = state.sparklePhase;
        if (spT > 1.35) {
          sparkles.visible = false;
        } else {
          for (const c of sparkles.children) {
            c.position.set(
              c.userData.vx * spT,
              0.36 + c.userData.vy * spT - 1.2 * spT * spT,
              c.userData.vz * spT
            );
            c.rotation.y += dt * 8;
            c.scale.setScalar(Math.max(0.01, 1 - spT / 1.35));
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  clear() {
    for (const entry of this.props.values()) {
      this.group.remove(entry.mesh);
      entry.mesh.traverse((node) => {
        if (node.geometry) node.geometry.dispose();
        if (node.material) {
          const mats = Array.isArray(node.material) ? node.material : [node.material];
          for (const m of mats) {
            if (m.map) m.map.dispose();
            m.dispose();
          }
        }
      });
    }
    this.props.clear();
  }

  dispose() {
    this.clear();
    this.scene.remove(this.group);
  }
}

/**
 * Real 3D models & Mixamo FBX animation templates discovered in ./Assets.
 */
export const ASSET_MANIFEST = {
  house: {
    id: "granny_house",
    fbxPath: "./Assets/granny-1-house/source/GrannyHouse.fbx",
    textureDir: "./Assets/granny-1-house/textures/",
    textureAliases: {},
  },

  characters: {
    granny: {
      id: "granny",
      label: "Granny",
      fbxPath: "./Assets/granny-animated-fbx/source/GrannyTest6.fbx",
      textureDir: "./Assets/granny-animated-fbx/textures/",
      textureAliases: {
        // GrannyTest6.fbx references _Normal.png while disk file is _Norm.png
        "TexturesCom_WoodFine0077_6_seamless_S_Normal.png":
          "TexturesCom_WoodFine0077_6_seamless_S_Norm.png",
      },
      targetHeight: 1.65,
      // Raw GrannyTest6 faces +X; rotate -90 deg on Y so forward aligns with +Z
      forwardYawOffset: -Math.PI * 0.5,
      // Bake raw SkinnedMesh bindMatrix to its world transform before normalizing scale

================================================================
===== FILE: src/episodeRunner.js =====
================================================================
import * as THREE from "three";
import { CONFIG, SET_MAP, NAV_GRAPH } from "./config.js";

const LOCOMOTION_ANIMS = new Set(["walk", "run", "run2", "sneak", "granny_walk", "jump"]);
const PLAYER_PERSONAL_SPACE = 1.3;
const ACTOR_SEPARATION = 1.05;

const BASE_WALK_SPEED = CONFIG.baseWalkSpeed ?? 2.5;
const BASE_RUN_SPEED = CONFIG.baseRunSpeed ?? 3.8;
const BASE_SNEAK_SPEED = CONFIG.baseSneakSpeed ?? 1.65;
const BASE_STAIR_SPEED = CONFIG.baseStairSpeed ?? 1.85;
const BASE_JUMP_SPEED = CONFIG.baseJumpSpeed ?? 2.35;

function getFloorIndex(y) {
  return y < 2.1 ? 1 : 2;
}

function getBaseFloorY(y) {
  return getFloorIndex(y) === 1 ? 0.0 : 2.85;
}

function isOnStairs(pos) {
  return (
    pos.x >= -2.7 &&
    pos.x <= -1.1 &&
    pos.z >= 1.15 &&
    pos.z <= 4.15 &&
    pos.y >= 0.12 &&
    pos.y <= 2.75
  );
}

function isElevatedSurfacePos(pos) {
  if (isOnStairs(pos)) return false;
  const floorY = getBaseFloorY(pos.y);
  return Math.abs(pos.y - floorY) > 0.25;
}

/**
 * Deterministic 60Hz POV Episode & Multi-Skit Beat Runner.
 * Implements:
 *  - Connected indoor NavGraph + Dijkstra shortest-path routing (both floors + stairs + elevated furniture)
 *  - Dynamic distance-based movement duration (`distance / BASE_WALK_SPEED`)
 *  - True `async/await` Promise-based beat execution for `await: true` / `sync: "sequential"`
 *  - Y-axis sine-wave parabolic jump arcs onto/off elevated furniture waypoints
 */
export class EpisodeRunner {
  constructor({
    characters,
    playerCamera,
    cameraDirector = null,
    comedyProps,
    sfxEngine,
    house = null,
  }) {
    this.characters = characters;
    this.playerCamera = playerCamera;
    this.cameraDirector = cameraDirector;
    this.comedyProps = comedyProps;
    this.sfxEngine = sfxEngine;
    this.house = house;

    this.episode = null;
    this.events = [];
    this.activeCharMoves = [];
    this.nextMoveId = 1;

    this._runToken = 0;
    this._seekToken = 0;
    this._silentSeek = false;
    this.isAwaitingBeat = false;
    this._simWaiters = [];
    this._timelineWaiters = [];

    this.simTime = 0;
    this.timelineTime = 0;
    this.duration = 0;
    this.isPlaying = false;
    this.isFinished = false;
    this.nextEventIndex = 0;

    this.onStateChange = null;
    this.onFinish = null;

    this.resolver = {
      getActor: (id) => this.characters[id] || null,
      getProp: (id) => this.comedyProps?.getProp(id) || null,
      getWaypoint: (id) => (typeof id === "string" ? SET_MAP[id] || null : id),
    };
  }

  setHouse(house) {
    this.house = house;
    this.playerCamera?.setHouse(house);
  }

  resolveWaypoint(target) {
    if (!target) return { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
    if (typeof target === "string") {
      if (SET_MAP[target]) return { ...SET_MAP[target], _name: target };
      const actor = this.characters[target];
      if (actor) {
        return {
          x: actor.root.position.x,
          y: actor.root.position.y,
          z: actor.root.position.z,
        };
      }
      const prop = this.comedyProps?.getProp(target);
      if (prop) {
        return {
          x: prop.mesh.position.x,
          y: prop.mesh.position.y,
          z: prop.mesh.position.z,
        };
      }
      console.warn(`[EpisodeRunner] Unknown target "${target}"`);
      return { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
    }
    return {
      x: target.x ?? 0,
      y: target.y ?? 0,
      z: target.z ?? 0,
      elevated: Boolean(target.elevated),
      approach: target.approach,
      _name: target._name,
    };
  }

  findClosestNavNodeId(pos) {
    if (pos?._name && NAV_GRAPH[pos._name]) {
      return pos._name;
    }
    const wantFloor = getFloorIndex(pos.y ?? 0);
    const wantElevated = isElevatedSurfacePos(pos);

    let bestId = null;
    let bestDist = Infinity;
    for (const [id, node] of Object.entries(NAV_GRAPH)) {
      const floorDiff = getFloorIndex(node.y ?? 0) === wantFloor ? 0 : 100;
      const elevPenalty = Boolean(node.elevated) !== wantElevated ? 25 : 0;
      const d =
        Math.hypot((node.x ?? 0) - (pos.x ?? 0), (node.y ?? 0) - (pos.y ?? 0), (node.z ?? 0) - (pos.z ?? 0)) +
        floorDiff +
        elevPenalty;
      if (d < bestDist) {
        bestDist = d;
        bestId = id;
      }
    }
    return bestId || "front_door_view";
  }

  /**
   * Dijkstra shortest-path search over `NAV_GRAPH`.
   * Prevents intermediate routing across `elevated` furniture unless it is the start/goal.
   */
  findNavPath(startId, goalId) {
    if (!startId || !goalId) return [];
    if (startId === goalId) return [startId];
    if (!NAV_GRAPH[startId] || !NAV_GRAPH[goalId]) return [startId, goalId];

    const dist = new Map();
    const prev = new Map();
    const unvisited = new Set(Object.keys(NAV_GRAPH));

    for (const id of unvisited) {
      dist.set(id, Infinity);
    }
    dist.set(startId, 0);

    while (unvisited.size > 0) {
      let u = null;
      let bestD = Infinity;
      for (const id of unvisited) {
        const d = dist.get(id);
        if (d < bestD) {
          bestD = d;
          u = id;
        }
      }
      if (!u || bestD === Infinity || u === goalId) break;
      unvisited.delete(u);

      const nodeU = NAV_GRAPH[u];
      for (const v of nodeU.neighbors || []) {
        if (!unvisited.has(v)) continue;
        const nodeV = NAV_GRAPH[v];
        if (!nodeV) continue;
        // Do not walk up onto intermediate elevated furniture while traversing a room
        if (nodeV.elevated && v !== goalId && v !== startId) continue;

        const edgeLen = Math.hypot(
          (nodeV.x ?? 0) - (nodeU.x ?? 0),
          (nodeV.y ?? 0) - (nodeU.y ?? 0),
          (nodeV.z ?? 0) - (nodeU.z ?? 0)
        );
        const alt = bestD + edgeLen;
        if (alt < dist.get(v)) {
          dist.set(v, alt);
          prev.set(v, u);
        }
      }
    }

    if (dist.get(goalId) === Infinity) {
      return [startId, goalId];
    }

    const path = [];
    let cur = goalId;
    while (cur !== undefined) {
      path.unshift(cur);
      if (cur === startId) break;
      cur = prev.get(cur);
    }
    return path;
  }

  /**
   * Builds a 3D path + per-segment metadata (jump arcs vs stair ramps vs flat floor)
   * using Dijkstra pathfinding over `NAV_GRAPH`.
   */
  buildPathWithSegments(fromPos, toPos) {
    const startVec = new THREE.Vector3(fromPos.x ?? 0, fromPos.y ?? 0, fromPos.z ?? 0);
    const goalVec = new THREE.Vector3(toPos.x ?? 0, toPos.y ?? 0, toPos.z ?? 0);

    if (startVec.distanceTo(goalVec) < 0.05) {
      return { points: [startVec, goalVec], segments: [{ isJumpArc: false, arcHeight: 0 }] };
    }

    const startId = this.findClosestNavNodeId(fromPos);
    const goalId = this.findClosestNavNodeId(toPos);
    const nodeIds = this.findNavPath(startId, goalId);

    const chain = [{ pt: startVec.clone(), isJumpArc: false, arcHeight: 0 }];

    for (let i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i += 1) {
      const id = nodeIds[i];
      const node = NAV_GRAPH[id] || SET_MAP[id];
      if (!node) continue;
      const pt = new THREE.Vector3(node.x, node.y ?? 0, node.z);
      const prev = chain[chain.length - 1].pt;
      if (prev.distanceTo(pt) < 0.06) continue;

      const prevNode = i > 0 ? NAV_GRAPH[nodeIds[i - 1]] : null;
      const prevElevated = prevNode ? Boolean(prevNode.elevated) : isElevatedSurfacePos(prev);
      const curElevated = Boolean(node.elevated);
      const isJumpArc = prevElevated || curElevated;
      const arcHeight = isJumpArc
        ? Math.min(0.62, 0.30 + Math.abs(pt.y - prev.y) * 0.25)
        : 0;

      chain.push({ pt, isJumpArc, arcHeight });
    }

    const tail = chain[chain.length - 1].pt;
    if (tail.distanceTo(goalVec) >= 0.06) {
      const lastNode = NAV_GRAPH[nodeIds[nodeIds.length - 1]];
      const isJumpArc = Boolean(lastNode?.elevated) || isElevatedSurfacePos(goalVec);
      chain.push({
        pt: goalVec.clone(),
        isJumpArc,
        arcHeight: isJumpArc ? Math.min(0.62, 0.30 + Math.abs(goalVec.y - tail.y) * 0.25) : 0,
      });
    }

    return this._finalizeChain(chain);
  }

  _finalizeChain(chain) {
    const points = [chain[0].pt];
    const segments = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
      const prev = points[points.length - 1];
      const next = chain[i].pt;
      if (prev.distanceTo(next) < 0.06) continue;
      points.push(next);
      segments.push({
        isJumpArc: Boolean(chain[i].isJumpArc),
        arcHeight: chain[i].arcHeight || 0,
      });
    }
    if (points.length < 2) {
      points.push(chain[chain.length - 1].pt.clone());
      segments.push({ isJumpArc: false, arcHeight: 0 });
    }
    return { points, segments };
  }

  computePath(fromPos, toPos) {
    return this.buildPathWithSegments(fromPos, toPos).points;
  }

  _buildFullStopChain(from, stops) {
    const allPoints = [];
    const allSegments = [];
    let cursor = from;
    for (const stop of stops) {
      const dst = this.resolveWaypoint(stop);
      const { points, segments } = this.buildPathWithSegments(cursor, dst);
      if (allPoints.length === 0) {
        allPoints.push(...points);
        allSegments.push(...segments);
      } else {
        for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i += 1) {
          const prev = allPoints[allPoints.length - 1];
          if (prev.distanceTo(points[i]) >= 0.06) {
            allPoints.push(points[i]);
            allSegments.push(segments[i - 1]);
          }
        }
      }
      cursor = dst;
    }
    if (allPoints.length < 2) {
      const p0 = new THREE.Vector3(from.x ?? 0, from.y ?? 0, from.z ?? 0);
      return {
        points: [p0, p0.clone()],
        segments: [{ isJumpArc: false, arcHeight: 0 }],
      };
    }
    return { points: allPoints, segments: allSegments };
  }

  _computeMoveTiming(
    points,
    segments,
    requestedDuration,
    requestedAnim,
    isPlayer = false,
    customSpeed = null
  ) {
    const isRun = requestedAnim === "run" || requestedAnim === "run2";
    const isSneak = requestedAnim === "sneak";
    const baseFlatSpeed =
      Number(customSpeed) > 0
        ? Number(customSpeed)
        : isPlayer
          ? BASE_WALK_SPEED
          : isRun
            ? BASE_RUN_SPEED
            : isSneak
              ? BASE_SNEAK_SPEED
              : BASE_WALK_SPEED;
    const stairSpeed = Math.min(baseFlatSpeed, isRun ? 2.45 : BASE_STAIR_SPEED);
    const maxVertSpeed = isPlayer ? 1.55 : isRun ? 1.75 : 1.4;

    const cumWeights = [0];
    let totalWeight = 0;
    let totalLength = 0;
    const segInfo = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i += 1) {
      const a = points[i - 1];
      const b = points[i];
      const segLen = a.distanceTo(b);
      const dy = Math.abs(b.y - a.y);
      const meta = segments[i - 1] || { isJumpArc: false, arcHeight: 0 };
      const isStair = !meta.isJumpArc && dy > 0.15;

      let minSegDur;
      if (meta.isJumpArc) {
        minSegDur = Math.max(0.55, segLen / Math.max(baseFlatSpeed, BASE_JUMP_SPEED));
      } else if (isStair) {
        minSegDur = Math.max(segLen / stairSpeed, dy / maxVertSpeed);
      } else {
        minSegDur = segLen / baseFlatSpeed;
      }

      totalLength += segLen;
      totalWeight += Math.max(1e-4, minSegDur);
      cumWeights.push(totalWeight);
      segInfo.push({
        ...meta,
        isStair,
        segLen,
        minSegDur,
      });
    }

    // Distance / BASE_WALK_SPEED is the lower bound; never teleport faster than physical walk speed
    const explicitMin = Number(requestedDuration) > 0 ? Number(requestedDuration) : 0;
    const effectiveDuration = Math.max(0.08, totalWeight, explicitMin);
    return {
      effectiveDuration,
      cumWeights,
      totalWeight,
      totalLength,
      segInfo,
      maxVertSpeed,
      baseFlatSpeed,
    };
  }

  compileTimeline(episode) {
    const rawEvents = [];

    if (Array.isArray(episode.skits)) {
      let skitCursor = 0;
      for (const skit of episode.skits) {
        let beatCursor = skit.startAt ?? skitCursor;
        let skitMaxEnd = beatCursor;
        const skitSeq = skit.sync === "sequential";

        for (const beat of skit.beats || []) {
          let at = beatCursor;
          if (typeof beat.at === "number") {
            at = beat.at;
          } else if (typeof beat.after === "number") {
            at = beatCursor + beat.after;
          }
          const duration = Math.max(0, beat.duration ?? 0);
          const isAwait =
            beat.await === true || beat.sync === "sequential" || skitSeq;
          rawEvents.push({
            ...beat,
            await: isAwait || beat.await === true,
            at,
            duration,
          });
          // Awaited beats freeze timelineTime in _driveTimelineAsync while awaiting their Promise,
          // so beatCursor stays at `at` (preventing double-counting `duration` on `after:` offsets).
          beatCursor = at;
          skitMaxEnd = Math.max(skitMaxEnd, at + (isAwait ? 0 : duration));
        }
        skitCursor = skitMaxEnd;
      }
    } else if (Array.isArray(episode.actions)) {
      for (const act of episode.actions) {
        rawEvents.push({
          ...act,
          at: act.at ?? 0,
          duration: Math.max(0, act.duration ?? 0),
        });
      }
    }

    rawEvents.sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at);
    return rawEvents;
  }

  _estimateEpisodeDuration(episode) {
    const actorPos = {};
    for (const castEntry of episode.cast || []) {
      const sp = this.resolveWaypoint(castEntry.spawn || "back_hall");
      actorPos[castEntry.id] = { ...sp };
    }
    const pSp = this.resolveWaypoint(episode.playerSpawn || "back_hall");
    let playerPos = { ...pSp };

    let tlShift = 0;
    let maxEnd = 1.0;

    for (const ev of this.events) {
      const fireSim = ev.at + tlShift;

      let effDur = Math.max(0, ev.duration ?? 0);
      if (ev.type === "player_walk") {
        const stops = [...(ev.via || []), ev.to];
        const { points, segments } = this._buildFullStopChain(playerPos, stops);
        const timing = this._computeMoveTiming(
          points,
          segments,
          ev.duration,
          "walk",
          true,
          ev.speed
        );
        effDur = timing.effectiveDuration;
        const last = points[points.length - 1];
        const lastStop = stops[stops.length - 1];
        playerPos = {
          x: last.x,
          y: last.y,
          z: last.z,
          _name: typeof lastStop === "string" ? lastStop : undefined,
        };
      } else if (ev.type === "char_move" || ev.type === "move") {
        const start = ev.from
          ? this.resolveWaypoint(ev.from)
          : actorPos[ev.actor] || { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
        const stops = [...(ev.via || []), ev.to];
        const { points, segments } = this._buildFullStopChain(start, stops);
        const timing = this._computeMoveTiming(
          points,
          segments,
          ev.duration,
          ev.anim || "walk",
          false,
          ev.speed
        );
        effDur = timing.effectiveDuration;
        const last = points[points.length - 1];
        const lastStop = stops[stops.length - 1];
        actorPos[ev.actor] = {
          x: last.x,
          y: last.y,
          z: last.z,
          _name: typeof lastStop === "string" ? lastStop : undefined,
        };
      } else if ((ev.type === "char_anim" || ev.type === "anim") && effDur === 0) {
        effDur = ev.loop ? 0.45 : 1.0;
      }

      maxEnd = Math.max(maxEnd, fireSim + effDur);
      if (ev.await === true || ev.sync === "sequential") {
        tlShift += effDur;
      }
    }

    return maxEnd;
  }

  loadEpisode(episodeJson) {
    const episode =
      typeof episodeJson === "string" ? JSON.parse(episodeJson) : episodeJson;

    this.episode = episode;
    this.events = this.compileTimeline(episode);
    this.duration = this._estimateEpisodeDuration(episode);
    this.reset();
    return {
      title: episode.title ?? "Untitled Episode",
      duration: this.duration,
      eventCount: this.events.length,
    };
  }

  _clearWaiters() {
    for (const m of this.activeCharMoves) {
      if (m.resolve) m.resolve();
    }
    this.activeCharMoves = [];
    for (const w of this._simWaiters) {
      if (w.resolve) w.resolve();
    }
    this._simWaiters = [];
    for (const w of this._timelineWaiters) {
      if (w.resolve) w.resolve();
    }
    this._timelineWaiters = [];
    this.isAwaitingBeat = false;
  }

  _waitSim(duration, runToken) {
    if (duration <= 1e-5) return Promise.resolve();
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this._simWaiters.push({
        targetTime: this.simTime + duration,
        runToken,
        resolve,
      });
    });
  }

  _waitTimelineUntil(targetTimelineTime, runToken) {
    if (this.timelineTime >= targetTimelineTime - 1e-6) {
      return Promise.resolve();
    }
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this._timelineWaiters.push({
        targetTime: targetTimelineTime,
        runToken,
        resolve,
      });
    });
  }

  async _flushMicrotasks() {
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
      await Promise.resolve();
    }
  }

  /**
   * True `async / await` timeline scheduler.
   * For every beat with `await: true` or `sync: "sequential"`, awaits the beat's
   * Promise (movement, camera walk, look, animation, or timer) before advancing.
   */
  async _driveTimelineAsync(runToken) {
    while (runToken === this._runToken && this.nextEventIndex < this.events.length) {
      const ev = this.events[this.nextEventIndex];
      if (ev.at > this.timelineTime + 1e-6) {
        await this._waitTimelineUntil(ev.at, runToken);
      }
      if (runToken !== this._runToken) return;
      this.nextEventIndex += 1;
      const beatPromise = this.executeBeat(ev, {
        silentSeek: this._silentSeek,
        runToken,
      });
      if (ev.await === true || ev.sync === "sequential") {
        this.isAwaitingBeat = true;
        await beatPromise;
        if (runToken !== this._runToken) return;
        this.isAwaitingBeat = false;
        
        // CRITICAL FIX: Shift all future events forward by the time we spent waiting
        // so they don't fire instantly all at once.
        const shiftAmount = this.simTime - this.timelineTime;
        if (shiftAmount > 0) {
          for (let i = this.nextEventIndex; i < this.events.length; i++) {
            this.events[i].at += shiftAmount;
          }
          this.timelineTime = this.simTime;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  reset() {
    this._runToken += 1;
    const runToken = this._runToken;
    this._clearWaiters();

    this.simTime = 0;
    this.timelineTime = 0;
    this.nextEventIndex = 0;
    this.nextMoveId = 1;
    this.isPlaying = false;
    this.isFinished = false;
    this.sfxEngine?.stopAll();
    this.sfxEngine?.resetPacerClock(0);

    if (!this.episode) return;
    this.events = this.compileTimeline(this.episode);

    if (this.episode.aspect && this.playerCamera) {
      this.playerCamera.setAspect(this.episode.aspect);
    }

    for (const char of Object.values(this.characters)) {
      char.resetPose();
      char.root.visible = false;
    }

    for (const castEntry of this.episode.cast || []) {
      const char = this.characters[castEntry.id];
      if (!char) continue;
      char.root.visible = true;
      const spawn = this.resolveWaypoint(castEntry.spawn || "back_hall");
      char.root.position.set(spawn.x, spawn.y ?? 0, spawn.z);

      if (castEntry.facing) {
        const facePt = this.resolveWaypoint(castEntry.facing);
        char.lookAt(facePt);
      } else {
        char.root.rotation.y = 0;
      }
      char.playAnim(castEntry.anim || "idle", 0.01, null, true);
      char.mixer.update(0.016);
    }

    this.comedyProps?.loadProps(this.episode.props || []);

    if (this.playerCamera) {
      const pSpawn = this.resolveWaypoint(this.episode.playerSpawn || "back_hall");
      const pLook = this.episode.playerFacing
        ? this.resolveWaypoint(this.episode.playerFacing)
        : null;
      const lookVec = pLook
        ? new THREE.Vector3(pLook.x, (pLook.y ?? 0) + 1.35, pLook.z)
        : null;
      this.playerCamera.reset(pSpawn, lookVec);
      this.playerCamera.setInspectionMode(false);
    }

    this._driveTimelineAsync(runToken);
    this.playerCamera?.update(0, this.resolver);
    this.emitState();
  }

  play() {
    if (!this.episode) return;
    if (this.isFinished) this.reset();
    this.isPlaying = true;
    this.emitState();
  }

  pause() {
    this.isPlaying = false;
    this.sfxEngine?.stopAll();
    this.emitState();
  }

  stop() {
    this.isPlaying = false;
    this.reset();
  }

  async seek(targetTime) {
    if (!this.episode) return;
    const seekToken = ++this._seekToken;
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.duration, targetTime));
    const wasPlaying = this.isPlaying;
    const wasExport = this.sfxEngine?.exportMode;

    if (this.sfxEngine) {
      this.sfxEngine.exportMode = true;
      const savedEvents = [...this.sfxEngine.recordedEvents];
      this._silentSeek = true;
      this.reset();
      await this._flushMicrotasks();
      const dt = CONFIG.fixedDt;
      const steps = Math.floor(clamped / dt);
      for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
        if (seekToken !== this._seekToken) break;
        await this.stepAsync(dt, { silentSeek: true });
      }
      const rem = clamped - steps * dt;
      if (rem > 1e-5 && seekToken === this._seekToken) {
        await this.stepAsync(rem, { silentSeek: true });
      }
      this._silentSeek = false;
      this.sfxEngine.recordedEvents = savedEvents;
      this.sfxEngine.exportMode = Boolean(wasExport);
    }

    if (seekToken === this._seekToken) {
      this.isPlaying = wasPlaying;
      this.emitState();
    }
  }

  async stepAsync(dt, { silentSeek = false } = {}) {
    this.step(dt, { silentSeek });
    await this._flushMicrotasks();
  }

  _resolveReadyWaiters() {
    for (let i = this._simWaiters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const w = this._simWaiters[i];
      if (w.runToken !== this._runToken || this.simTime >= w.targetTime - 1e-6) {
        this._simWaiters.splice(i, 1);
        w.resolve();
      }
    }
    for (let i = this._timelineWaiters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const w = this._timelineWaiters[i];
      if (w.runToken !== this._runToken || this.timelineTime >= w.targetTime - 1e-6) {
        this._timelineWaiters.splice(i, 1);
        w.resolve();
      }
    }
  }

  step(dt, { silentSeek = false } = {}) {
    if (!this.episode) return;

    this.simTime += dt;

    if (!this.isAwaitingBeat) {
      this.timelineTime += dt;
    } else if (this.simTime > this.duration) {
      this.duration = this.simTime + 0.05;
    }

    // 1. Advance character 3D polyline moves (with sine-wave jump arcs & stair slope clamping)
    let anyCharMoving = false;
    let fastestMoveAnim = "walk";
    for (let i = this.activeCharMoves.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const m = this.activeCharMoves[i];
      const prevY = m.actor.root.position.y;
      const t =
        m.duration <= 0
          ? 1
          : Math.min(1, (this.simTime - m.startTime) / m.duration);
      const targetWeight = m.totalWeight * t;

      let segIdx = 0;
      while (
        segIdx < m.cumWeights.length - 2 &&
        m.cumWeights[segIdx + 1] < targetWeight
      ) {
        segIdx++;
      }

      const segStart = m.points[segIdx];
      const segEnd = m.points[segIdx + 1];
      const segSpan = Math.max(
        1e-5,
        m.cumWeights[segIdx + 1] - m.cumWeights[segIdx]
      );
      const localT = Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(1, (targetWeight - m.cumWeights[segIdx]) / segSpan)
      );
      const segMeta = m.segInfo[segIdx] || {};

      m.actor.root.position.lerpVectors(segStart, segEnd, localT);

      if (segMeta.isJumpArc) {
        m.actor.root.position.y +=
          Math.sin(localT * Math.PI) * (segMeta.arcHeight || 0.44);
      } else if (segMeta.isStair) {
        const maxStepY = (m.maxVertSpeed || 1.55) * Math.max(dt, 1 / 120) * 1.35;
        const dyStep = m.actor.root.position.y - prevY;
        if (Math.abs(dyStep) > maxStepY) {
          m.actor.root.position.y = prevY + Math.sign(dyStep) * maxStepY;
        }
      } else {
        // Strict flat floor lock between same-floor standard nodes
        m.actor.root.position.y =
          Math.abs(segEnd.y - segStart.y) < 0.05
            ? segStart.y
            : segStart.y + (segEnd.y - segStart.y) * localT;
      }

      const dx = segEnd.x - segStart.x;
      const dz = segEnd.z - segStart.z;
      if (dx * dx + dz * dz > 1e-6 && !m.keepFacing) {
        m.actor.faceTarget(segEnd, 14);
      }

      const desiredAnim = segMeta.isJumpArc ? "jump" : m.anim;
      const act = m.actor.currentAction;
      if (segMeta.isJumpArc) {
        if (m.actor.currentAnimation !== "jump") {
          m.actor.playAnim("jump", 0.06, 1.15, false);
        }
      } else if (
        m.actor.currentAnimation !== desiredAnim ||
        !act ||
        act.paused ||
        act.loop !== THREE.LoopRepeat
      ) {
        m.actor.playAnim(desiredAnim, 0.08, m.animSpeed, true);
      }

      anyCharMoving = true;
      if (m.anim === "run" || m.anim === "run2") fastestMoveAnim = m.anim;

      if (t >= 1) {
        m.actor.root.position.copy(m.points[m.points.length - 1]);
        m.actor.playAnim(m.endAnim || "idle", 0.2, null, true);
        this.activeCharMoves.splice(i, 1);
        if (m.resolve) m.resolve();
      }
    }

    // 2. Prevent standing ground-level actors from merging/clipping inside each other
    const visibleActors = Object.values(this.characters).filter((c) => c.root.visible);
    const movingActorSet = new Set(this.activeCharMoves.map((m) => m.actor));
    for (let i = 0; i < visibleActors.length; i += 1) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < visibleActors.length; j += 1) {
        const actorA = visibleActors[i];
        const actorB = visibleActors[j];
        if (movingActorSet.has(actorA) || movingActorSet.has(actorB)) continue;
        const a = actorA.root.position;
        const b = actorB.root.position;
        if (Math.abs(a.y - b.y) > 0.55) continue;
        if (isElevatedSurfacePos(a) || isElevatedSurfacePos(b)) continue;
        const dx = b.x - a.x;
        const dz = b.z - a.z;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
        if (dist > 1e-4 && dist < ACTOR_SEPARATION) {
          const push = (ACTOR_SEPARATION - dist) * 0.5;
          const nx = dx / dist;
          const nz = dz / dist;
          const ax = a.x - nx * push;
          const az = a.z - nz * push;
          const bx = b.x + nx * push;
          const bz = b.z + nz * push;
          if (!this.house || a.y >= 0.4 || this.house.isPointInsideWalkable(ax, az)) {
            a.x = ax;
            a.z = az;
          }
          if (!this.house || b.y >= 0.4 || this.house.isPointInsideWalkable(bx, bz)) {
            b.x = bx;
            b.z = bz;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Advance PlayerCamera & smoothly ease personal space around same-floor standing actors
    this.playerCamera?.update(dt, this.resolver);
    if (this.playerCamera && this.playerCamera.mode === "pov") {
      const pPos = this.playerCamera.playerPos;
      if (!this.playerCamera.isWalking && !isElevatedSurfacePos(pPos) && !isOnStairs(pPos)) {
        const pushAlpha = Math.min(1, dt * 6);
        for (const actor of visibleActors) {
          if (movingActorSet.has(actor)) continue;
          if (Math.abs(pPos.y - actor.root.position.y) > 0.65) continue;
          const dx = pPos.x - actor.root.position.x;
          const dz = pPos.z - actor.root.position.z;
          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
          if (dist > 1e-4 && dist < PLAYER_PERSONAL_SPACE) {
            const nx = dx / dist;
            const nz = dz / dist;
            const targetX = actor.root.position.x + nx * PLAYER_PERSONAL_SPACE;
            const targetZ = actor.root.position.z + nz * PLAYER_PERSONAL_SPACE;
            const candX = pPos.x + (targetX - pPos.x) * pushAlpha;
            const candZ = pPos.z + (targetZ - pPos.z) * pushAlpha;
            if (!this.house || pPos.y >= 0.4 || this.house.isPointInsideWalkable(candX, candZ)) {
              pPos.x = candX;
              pPos.z = candZ;
              this.playerCamera.camera.position.x = candX;
              this.playerCamera.camera.position.z = candZ;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    const playerMoving = Boolean(this.playerCamera?.isWalking);

    // 4. Update Characters
    const playerCam = this.playerCamera?.camera || null;
    const playerCamPos = playerCam?.position || null;
    for (const char of visibleActors) {
      char.update(dt, playerCamPos, playerCam);
    }

    // 5. Update 3D Comedy Props
    this.comedyProps?.update(dt, playerCamPos);

    // 6. Advance SFX Pacer & movement footsteps
    if (!silentSeek && this.sfxEngine) {
      const anyMoving = playerMoving || anyCharMoving;
      const moveAnim = playerMoving ? "walk" : fastestMoveAnim;
      this.sfxEngine.tickPacer(dt, this.simTime, { anyMoving, moveAnim });
    }

    // 7. Resolve timeline & simulation-time Promises ready on this tick
    this._resolveReadyWaiters();

    // 8. Check completion (only finish when all events dispatched and no awaited beat active)
    if (
      this.nextEventIndex >= this.events.length &&
      !this.isAwaitingBeat &&
      this.activeCharMoves.length === 0 &&
      !this.playerCamera?.isWalking &&
      this.simTime >= this.duration - 1e-5 &&
      !this.isFinished
    ) {
      this.simTime = this.duration;
      this.isFinished = true;
      this.isPlaying = false;
      this.sfxEngine?.stopBgm(this.simTime);
      this.emitState();
      if (this.onFinish) this.onFinish();
    } else {
      this.emitState();
    }
  }

  async executeBeat(ev, { silentSeek = false, runToken = this._runToken } = {}) {
    const type = ev.type;

    if (type === "player_walk" && this.playerCamera) {
      const from = {
        x: this.playerCamera.playerPos.x,
        y: this.playerCamera.playerPos.y,
        z: this.playerCamera.playerPos.z,
      };
      const stops = [...(ev.via || []), ev.to];
      const { points, segments } = this._buildFullStopChain(from, stops);
      return this.playerCamera.startWalk({
        points,
        segments,
        duration: ev.duration,
        speed: ev.speed,
        autoLook: ev.lookAhead !== false,
      });
    }

    if (type === "player_look" && this.playerCamera) {
      const dur = ev.duration ?? 0.2;
      const lookPromise = this.playerCamera.lookAt(
        ev.target,
        { duration: dur, track: Boolean(ev.track) },
        this.resolver
      );
      if (!silentSeek) {
        this.sfxEngine?.notifyWhipPan(dur, this.simTime);
      }
      return lookPromise;
    }

    if (type === "player_gesture" && this.playerCamera) {
      return this.playerCamera.triggerGesture(ev.gesture, ev.duration ?? 0.8);
    }

    if (type === "player_crouch" && this.playerCamera) {
      return this.playerCamera.crouch({
        crouched: Boolean(ev.crouched),
        duration: ev.duration ?? 0.35,
      });
    }

    if (type === "player_hold" && this.playerCamera) {
      if (ev.prop !== undefined && ev.prop !== this.playerCamera.heldPropType) {
        this.playerCamera.holdProp(ev.prop ?? null);
        if (!silentSeek && ev.prop) {
          this.sfxEngine?.notifyPickup(ev.prop, this.simTime);
        }
      }
      if (ev.use) {
        this.playerCamera.useHeldProp();
      }
      return this._waitSim(ev.duration ?? 0, runToken);
    }

    if (type === "fade" && this.playerCamera) {
      return this.playerCamera.startFade({
        to: ev.to ?? 0,
        duration: ev.duration ?? 0.6,
      });
    }

    if (type === "char_anim" || type === "anim") {
      const actor = this.characters[ev.actor];
      if (!actor) return;
      actor.playAnim(
        ev.anim,
        ev.fadeDuration ?? 0.16,
        ev.speed ?? null,
        ev.loop !== undefined ? ev.loop : null
      );
      if (!silentSeek) {
        this.sfxEngine?.notifyAction(ev.anim, this.simTime);
      }
      const clipDur = actor.currentAction?.getClip()?.duration || 1.0;
      const waitDur = ev.duration ?? (ev.loop ? 0.45 : clipDur);
      return this._waitSim(waitDur, runToken);
    }

    if (type === "char_move" || type === "move") {
      const actor = this.characters[ev.actor];
      if (!actor) return;

      // Cancel any prior active move for the same actor cleanly
      for (let i = this.activeCharMoves.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (this.activeCharMoves[i].actor === actor) {
          if (this.activeCharMoves[i].resolve) this.activeCharMoves[i].resolve();
          this.activeCharMoves.splice(i, 1);
        }
      }

      const from = ev.from
        ? this.resolveWaypoint(ev.from)
        : {
            x: actor.root.position.x,
            y: actor.root.position.y,
            z: actor.root.position.z,
          };
      const stops = [...(ev.via || []), ev.to];
      const { points, segments } = this._buildFullStopChain(from, stops);

      const requestedAnim = ev.anim || "walk";
      const anim = LOCOMOTION_ANIMS.has(requestedAnim) ? requestedAnim : "walk";
      const timing = this._computeMoveTiming(
        points,
        segments,
        ev.duration,
        anim,
        false,
        ev.speed
      );

      const worldSpeed = timing.totalLength / timing.effectiveDuration;
      const animSpeed =
        ev.speed ?? THREE.MathUtils.clamp(worldSpeed / 1.35, 0.85, 1.75);

      if (!ev.keepFacing) {
        actor.lookAtPlayer(false);
      }
      actor.root.position.set(from.x, from.y ?? 0, from.z);
      actor.playAnim(anim, 0.14, animSpeed, true);

      const moveId = this.nextMoveId++;
      return new Promise((resolve) => {
        this.activeCharMoves.push({
          moveId,
          actor,
          points,
          cumWeights: timing.cumWeights,
          totalWeight: timing.totalWeight,
          segInfo: timing.segInfo,
          maxVertSpeed: timing.maxVertSpeed,
          anim,
          animSpeed,
          keepFacing: Boolean(ev.keepFacing),
          endAnim: ev.endAnim || "idle",
          startTime: this.simTime,
          duration: timing.effectiveDuration,
          resolve,
        });
      });
    }

    if (type === "char_face" || type === "face") {
      const actor = this.characters[ev.actor];
      if (!actor) return;
      const speed = ev.instant ? 999 : 14;
      if (typeof ev.followPlayer === "boolean") {
        actor.lookAtPlayer(ev.followPlayer);
        if (ev.followPlayer && ev.instant && this.playerCamera) {
          actor.lookAt(this.playerCamera.camera.position);
        }
      }
      if (ev.target === "player" && this.playerCamera) {
        actor.faceTarget(this.playerCamera.camera.position, speed);
      } else if (ev.target) {
        actor.faceTarget(this.resolveWaypoint(ev.target), speed);
      }
      return this._waitSim(ev.duration ?? 0, runToken);
    }

    if (type === "char_bubble") {
      const actor = this.characters[ev.actor];
      if (!actor) return;
      actor.showBubble(ev.icon || "question", ev.duration ?? 2.0);
      if (!silentSeek) {
        this.sfxEngine?.notifyBubblePop(ev.icon, this.simTime);
      }
      return this._waitSim(ev.duration ?? 0, runToken);
    }

    if (type === "char_prop") {
      const actor = this.characters[ev.actor];
      if (!actor) return;
      actor.setProp(ev.prop ?? null);
      return this._waitSim(ev.duration ?? 0, runToken);
    }

    if (type === "prop_action") {
      this.comedyProps?.performAction(ev.prop, ev.action, {
        at: ev.waypoint,
        to: ev.to,
        travelDuration: ev.travelDuration,
        targetY: ev.targetY,
        duration: ev.duration,
        yOffset: ev.yOffset ?? 0.25,
      });
      return this._waitSim(ev.duration ?? 0, runToken);
    }

    if (type === "bgm" && !silentSeek) {
      this.sfxEngine?.playBgm(ev.sound, {
        volume: ev.volume ?? 0.24,
        simTime: this.simTime,
      });
      return;
    }

    if (type === "stop_bgm" && !silentSeek) {
      this.sfxEngine?.stopBgm(this.simTime);
      return;
    }

    if (type === "sfx" && !silentSeek) {
      this.sfxEngine?.play(ev.sound, {
        volume: ev.volume,
        pitch: ev.pitch ?? 1.0,
        simTime: this.simTime,
        cutBgm: Boolean(ev.cutBgm),
        cutBgmDuration: ev.cutBgmDuration ?? 1.4,
      });
    }

    if ((ev.duration ?? 0) > 0) {
      return this._waitSim(ev.duration, runToken);
    }
  }

  emitState() {
    if (this.onStateChange) {
      this.onStateChange({
        simTime: this.simTime,
        duration: this.duration,
        isPlaying: this.isPlaying,
        isFinished: this.isFinished,
      });
    }
  }
}


================================================================
===== FILE: src/main.js =====
================================================================
import * as THREE from "three";
import { CONFIG, DEFAULT_EPISODE, EPISODE_PRESETS, SET_MAP } from "./config.js";
import {
  loadHouse,
  loadCharacterTemplate,
  instantiateCharacter,
} from "./assetLoader.js";
import { AnimationLibrary } from "./animationLibrary.js";
import { SfxEngine } from "./sfxEngine.js";
import { PlayerCamera } from "./playerCamera.js";
import { ComedyPropManager, ThoughtBubbleCache } from "./comedyProps.js";
import { EpisodeRunner } from "./episodeRunner.js";
import { StudioUI } from "./studioUI.js";

const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
const isHeadless = urlParams.get("headless") === "1";

const loadingOverlay = document.getElementById("loadingOverlay");
const loadingText = document.getElementById("loadingText");
const loadingBar = document.getElementById("loadingBar");
const stageContainer = document.getElementById("stageContainer");
const fadeOverlay = document.getElementById("fadeOverlay");

function setProgress(pct, label) {
  if (loadingBar) loadingBar.style.width = `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct))}%`;
  if (loadingText) loadingText.textContent = label;
}

function setupCartoonLighting(scene) {
  scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x87cefa);
  scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x9fd8ff, 0.014);

  const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xfffbe6, 0x998877, 2.05);
  scene.add(hemi);

  const ambient = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xfff4e0, 0.98);
  scene.add(ambient);

  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff8e7, 1.35);
  sun.position.set(10, 18, 12);
  sun.castShadow = true;
  sun.shadow.mapSize.set(isHeadless ? 512 : 1024, isHeadless ? 512 : 1024);
  sun.shadow.camera.near = 1;
  sun.shadow.camera.far = 45;
  sun.shadow.camera.left = -12;
  sun.shadow.camera.right = 12;
  sun.shadow.camera.top = 12;
  sun.shadow.camera.bottom = -12;
  sun.shadow.bias = -0.0008;
  scene.add(sun);

  const roomLights = [
    { pos: [0.0, 2.3, -2.5], color: 0xfff1d6, intensity: 1.75, dist: 11 }, // Back Hall
    { pos: [4.8, 2.3, 1.5], color: 0xffeacc, intensity: 1.85, dist: 11 },  // Living Room
    { pos: [-5.2, 2.3, 0.5], color: 0xfff5e0, intensity: 1.85, dist: 11 }, // Kitchen
    { pos: [-0.8, 2.3, 0.2], color: 0xfff0d4, intensity: 1.45, dist: 10 }, // Foyer
    { pos: [-4.2, 4.9, 2.1], color: 0xfff4de, intensity: 1.65, dist: 11 }, // Upstairs Bedroom
  ];

  for (const rl of roomLights) {
    const pt = new THREE.PointLight(rl.color, rl.intensity, rl.dist, 1.15);
    pt.position.set(rl.pos[0], rl.pos[1], rl.pos[2]);
    scene.add(pt);
  }

  const camFill = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff6ea, 2.0, 7.5, 1.1);
  scene.add(camFill);
  return { camFill };
}

function getStageTargetSize(aspectKey) {
  const spec = CONFIG.aspectModes[aspectKey] || CONFIG.aspectModes["16:9"];
  if (isHeadless) {
    return { width: spec.width, height: spec.height };
  }
  const rect = stageContainer.getBoundingClientRect();
  const availW = Math.max(320, rect.width - 16);
  const availH = Math.max(240, rect.height - 16);
  const targetRatio = spec.aspect;
  let w = availW;
  let h = w / targetRatio;
  if (h > availH) {
    h = availH;
    w = h * targetRatio;
  }
  return { width: Math.floor(w), height: Math.floor(h) };
}

async function bootstrap() {
  setProgress(5, "Initializing Three.js POV renderer...");

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const { camFill } = setupCartoonLighting(scene);

  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
    antialias: !isHeadless,
    powerPreference: "high-performance",
    preserveDrawingBuffer: true,
  });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(isHeadless ? 1 : Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
  renderer.shadowMap.type = isHeadless ? THREE.BasicShadowMap : THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
  renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.24;
  renderer.autoClear = false;

  const initialSize = getStageTargetSize(CONFIG.defaultAspect);
  renderer.setSize(initialSize.width, initialSize.height, false);
  renderer.domElement.className = "studio-canvas";
  stageContainer.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

  const playerCamera = new PlayerCamera(renderer.domElement);
  scene.add(playerCamera.camera);

  // Dedicated fullscreen fade pass rendered after world + FPS viewmodel
  const fadeScene = new THREE.Scene();
  const fadeCamera = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-1, 1, 1, -1, 0, 1);
  const fadeQuadMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
    color: 0x000000,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0,
    depthTest: false,
    depthWrite: false,
  });
  const fadeQuad = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2), fadeQuadMat);
  fadeScene.add(fadeQuad);

  const comedyProps = new ComedyPropManager(scene, SET_MAP);
  const bubbleCache = new ThoughtBubbleCache();
  const sfxEngine = new SfxEngine();
  const animLib = new AnimationLibrary();

  setProgress(15, "Loading Granny 1 House, Cast & Mixamo templates...");
  const [house, , grannyTemplate, grandpaTemplate] = await Promise.all([
    loadHouse(),
    animLib.loadAllTemplates(),
    loadCharacterTemplate("granny"),
    loadCharacterTemplate("grandpa"),
  ]);

  scene.add(house.root);
  playerCamera.setHouse(house);

  if (!isHeadless) {
    setProgress(70, "Preloading 25 cartoon SFX & BGM...");
    await sfxEngine.preloadAll((done, total) => {
      setProgress(70 + (done / total) * 24, `Loading SFX (${done}/${total})...`);
    });
  }

  setProgress(95, "Retargeting animations for Granny & Grandpa...");
  const grannyClips = animLib.buildClipsForCharacter(grannyTemplate);
  const grandpaClips = animLib.buildClipsForCharacter(grandpaTemplate);
  animLib.disposeSourceTemplates();

  const granny = instantiateCharacter("granny", grannyTemplate, grannyClips, bubbleCache);
  const grandpa = instantiateCharacter("grandpa", grandpaTemplate, grandpaClips, bubbleCache);
  scene.add(granny.root);
  scene.add(grandpa.root);

  const characters = { granny, grandpa };

  const runner = new EpisodeRunner({
    characters,
    playerCamera,
    comedyProps,
    sfxEngine,
    house,
  });

  let ui = null;

  function resizeRenderer(aspectKey = playerCamera.aspectKey) {
    const { width, height } = getStageTargetSize(aspectKey);
    renderer.setSize(width, height, false);
    renderer.domElement.style.width = `${width}px`;
    renderer.domElement.style.height = `${height}px`;
    playerCamera.setAspect(aspectKey);
    playerCamera.camera.aspect = width / height;
    playerCamera.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    playerCamera.viewmodelCamera.aspect = width / height;
    playerCamera.viewmodelCamera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  }

  function loadEpisode(epObj) {
    const info = runner.loadEpisode(epObj);
    resizeRenderer(playerCamera.aspectKey);
    ui?.setEpisodeJson(epObj);
    ui?.syncButtons();
    ui?.syncState({
      simTime: runner.simTime,
      duration: runner.duration,
      isPlaying: runner.isPlaying,
      isFinished: runner.isFinished,
    });
    return info;
  }

  if (!isHeadless) {
    ui = new StudioUI({
      runner,
      sfxEngine,
      playerCamera,
      characters,
      renderer,
      onLoadEpisode: loadEpisode,
      onAspectChange: (key) => resizeRenderer(key),
    });

    runner.onStateChange = (st) => ui.syncState(st);
    runner.onFinish = () => {
      ui.syncButtons();
      ui.toast("Episode finished! Hit Play to replay or Edit JSON Skits.");
    };
  }

  window.addEventListener("resize", () => resizeRenderer(playerCamera.aspectKey));

  const heldKeys = new Set();
  window.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
    if (
      document.activeElement &&
      ["TEXTAREA", "INPUT", "SELECT"].includes(document.activeElement.tagName)
    ) {
      return;
    }
    heldKeys.add(e.code);
  });
  window.addEventListener("keyup", (e) => heldKeys.delete(e.code));

  const startPresetKey = urlParams.get("preset") || "grandpa_key_pov_44s";
  const initialEp = EPISODE_PRESETS[startPresetKey] || DEFAULT_EPISODE;
  loadEpisode(initialEp);

  if (isHeadless) {
    document.body.classList.add("headless-mode");
  }

  function renderFrame() {
    camFill.position.copy(playerCamera.camera.position);
    camFill.position.y = Math.max(0.8, playerCamera.camera.position.y + 0.1);

    const alpha = playerCamera.fadeAlpha;
    fadeQuadMat.opacity = alpha;
    if (fadeOverlay) {
      fadeOverlay.style.opacity = String(alpha);
    }

    renderer.clear();
    renderer.render(scene, playerCamera.camera);
    if (playerCamera.mode === "pov" && playerCamera.hasHeldProp()) {
      renderer.clearDepth();
      renderer.render(playerCamera.viewmodelScene, playerCamera.viewmodelCamera);
    }
    if (alpha > 0.002) {
      renderer.render(fadeScene, fadeCamera);
    }
  }

  renderFrame();
  setProgress(100, "Ready!");
  setTimeout(() => {
    loadingOverlay?.classList.add("hidden");
  }, 150);

  let lastWall = performance.now();
  let accumulator = 0;
  let liveStepBusy = false;

  async function animate(now) {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    if (isHeadless || liveStepBusy) return;

    const frameDt = Math.min(CONFIG.maxFrameDt, (now - lastWall) / 1000);
    lastWall = now;

    if (runner.isPlaying) {
      liveStepBusy = true;
      try {
        accumulator += frameDt;
        while (accumulator >= CONFIG.fixedDt) {
          await runner.stepAsync(CONFIG.fixedDt);
          accumulator -= CONFIG.fixedDt;
        }
      } finally {
        liveStepBusy = false;
      }
    } else if (heldKeys.size > 0 && playerCamera.mode === "pov") {
      const speed = 2.6 * frameDt;
      const forward = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1).applyEuler(
        new THREE.Euler(0, playerCamera.yaw, 0)
      );
      const right = new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0).applyEuler(
        new THREE.Euler(0, playerCamera.yaw, 0)
      );
      if (heldKeys.has("KeyW") || heldKeys.has("ArrowUp"))
        playerCamera.playerPos.addScaledVector(forward, speed);
      if (heldKeys.has("KeyS") || heldKeys.has("ArrowDown"))
        playerCamera.playerPos.addScaledVector(forward, -speed);
      if (heldKeys.has("KeyA") || heldKeys.has("ArrowLeft"))
        playerCamera.playerPos.addScaledVector(right, -speed);
      if (heldKeys.has("KeyD") || heldKeys.has("ArrowRight"))
        playerCamera.playerPos.addScaledVector(right, speed);
      if (heldKeys.has("KeyQ")) playerCamera.yaw += 1.6 * frameDt;
      if (heldKeys.has("KeyE")) playerCamera.yaw -= 1.6 * frameDt;
      playerCamera.update(frameDt, runner.resolver);
      ui?.syncState({
        simTime: runner.simTime,
        duration: runner.duration,
        isPlaying: false,
        isFinished: runner.isFinished,
      });
    } else if (playerCamera.mode === "orbit") {
      playerCamera.update(frameDt, runner.resolver);
    }

    renderFrame();
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Headless Deterministic Export Hook (used by scripts/export-video.mjs)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  window.__GRANNY_STUDIO__ = {
    version: "2.0.0-pov",
    ready: true,
    getPresetKeys: () => Object.keys(EPISODE_PRESETS),
    beginHeadlessExport: async ({ episode, pacerEnabled = true, aspectOverride = "" } = {}) => {
      sfxEngine.setPacerEnabled(pacerEnabled);
      sfxEngine.startEventCapture();
      if (episode) {
        runner.loadEpisode(episode);
      } else {
        runner.reset();
      }
      await runner._flushMicrotasks();
      if (aspectOverride && CONFIG.aspectModes[aspectOverride]) {
        resizeRenderer(aspectOverride);
      } else {
        resizeRenderer(playerCamera.aspectKey);
      }
      renderFrame();
      return {
        title: runner.episode?.title || "Granny POV Episode",
        duration: runner.duration,
        aspectKey: playerCamera.aspectKey,
        width: renderer.domElement.width,
        height: renderer.domElement.height,
      };
    },
    advanceExportFrames: async (frameCount, dt) => {
      if (frameCount > 0 && dt > 0) {
        const subSteps = Math.max(1, Math.round(dt / CONFIG.fixedDt));
        const subDt = dt / subSteps;
        for (let f = 0; f < frameCount; f += 1) {
          for (let i = 0; i < subSteps; i += 1) {
            await runner.stepAsync(subDt);
          }
        }
      }
      return {
        simTime: runner.simTime,
        isFinished: runner.isFinished,
      };
    },
    renderExportFrame: async (dt) => {
      if (dt > 0) {
        const subSteps = Math.max(1, Math.round(dt / CONFIG.fixedDt));
        const subDt = dt / subSteps;
        for (let i = 0; i < subSteps; i += 1) {
          await runner.stepAsync(subDt);
        }
      }
      renderFrame();
      return {
        simTime: runner.simTime,
        isFinished: runner.isFinished,
        dataUrl: renderer.domElement.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.90),
      };
    },
    finishHeadlessExport: () => {
      return sfxEngine.stopEventCapture(runner.simTime);
    },
  };
}

bootstrap().catch((err) => {
  console.error("[Studio] Fatal boot error:", err);
  window.__GRANNY_STUDIO_ERROR__ = err?.message || String(err);
  setProgress(100, `Error: ${err.message}`);
});


================================================================
===== FILE: src/playerCamera.js =====
================================================================
import * as THREE from "three";
import { OrbitControls } from "three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js";
import { CONFIG } from "./config.js";
import { createPropMesh } from "./comedyProps.js";

function clamp(v, min, max) {
  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));
}

// Lock FPS pitch cleanly between -38 deg (down) and +28 deg (up) so the camera frames rooms comfortably.
const MIN_CAMERA_PITCH = (-38 * Math.PI) / 180;
const MAX_CAMERA_PITCH = (28 * Math.PI) / 180;
const PLAYER_EYE_HEIGHT = CONFIG.playerEyeHeight ?? 1.55;
const BASE_WALK_SPEED = CONFIG.baseWalkSpeed ?? 2.5;
const BASE_STAIR_SPEED = CONFIG.baseStairSpeed ?? 1.85;
const BASE_JUMP_SPEED = CONFIG.baseJumpSpeed ?? 2.35;

function easeOutCubic(t) {
  return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
}

function easeInOutSine(t) {
  return -(Math.cos(Math.PI * t) - 1) / 2;
}

function wrapAngleDiff(target, current) {
  let diff = target - current;
  while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= Math.PI * 2;
  while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += Math.PI * 2;
  return diff;
}

/**
 * First-Person Player POV Camera Controller + Free Orbit Inspector toggle.
 * Supports eye-level walking with head-bob, whip-pan double-takes,
 * nod/shake/peek gestures, crouch/under-bed hiding, bottom-right FPS held-item
 * viewmodel, fade-to-black overlay, and 16:9 / 9:16 aspect toggling.
 */
export class PlayerCamera {
  constructor(domElement, house = null) {
    this.house = house;
    this.aspectKey = CONFIG.defaultAspect || "16:9";
    const aspectSpec = CONFIG.aspectModes[this.aspectKey];

    this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
      aspectSpec.fov,
      aspectSpec.aspect,
      0.06,
      120
    );
    this.camera.rotation.order = "YXZ";

    // Dedicated Viewmodel scene + camera rendered in a second depth-cleared pass
