    // so held FPS props NEVER clip into counters, walls, or doorways.
    this.viewmodelScene = new THREE.Scene();
    this.viewmodelCamera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
      aspectSpec.fov,
      aspectSpec.aspect,
      0.04,
      10
    );
    this.viewmodelScene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xfffbe6, 0x776655, 2.0));
    const vmKeyLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff8e7, 1.5);
    vmKeyLight.position.set(1.5, 2.5, 2.0);
    this.viewmodelScene.add(vmKeyLight);

    this.heldItemMount = new THREE.Group();
    this.heldItemMount.name = "FPSHeldItemMount";
    this.heldItemMount.position.set(0.28, -0.22, -0.5);
    this.viewmodelScene.add(this.heldItemMount);
    this.heldPropMesh = null;
    this.heldPropType = null;
    this.heldEquipPop = 1;
    this.heldUseTimer = 0;
    this.vmBobTime = 0;

    this.orbitControls = new OrbitControls(this.camera, domElement);
    this.orbitControls.enableDamping = true;
    this.orbitControls.dampingFactor = 0.08;
    this.orbitControls.target.set(0, 1.0, -2.0);
    this.orbitControls.enabled = false;

    this.mode = "pov"; // "pov" | "orbit"

    // Player spatial state
    this.playerPos = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -2.6); // Feet XZ on floor
    this.baseEyeHeight = CONFIG.playerEyeHeight;
    this.crouchStartHeight = CONFIG.playerEyeHeight;
    this.crouchTargetHeight = CONFIG.playerEyeHeight;
    this.crouchElapsed = 1;
    this.crouchDuration = 0.35;

    // Look yaw/pitch state
    this.yaw = 0;
    this.pitch = 0;
    this.lookStartYaw = 0;
    this.lookStartPitch = 0;
    this.lookTargetYaw = 0;
    this.lookTargetPitch = 0;
    this.lookElapsed = 1;
    this.lookDuration = 0.25;
    this.trackingRef = null;

    // Walk polyline state
    this.activeWalk = null;
    this.isWalking = false;
    this.bobPhase = 0;
    this.bobAmount = 0;

    // Gestures (nod_yes, shake_no, shake_panic, shake_earthquake, peek_lean)
    this.activeGesture = null;
    this.shakeIntensity = 0;
    this.shakePhase = 0;
    this.quakeTimer = 0;

    // Fade-to-black state
    this.fadeAlpha = 0;
    this.fadeStart = 0;
    this.fadeTarget = 0;
    this.fadeElapsed = 1;
    this.fadeDuration = 0.4;

    this._tmpTargetPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    this._tmpEyePos = new THREE.Vector3();
    this._tmpFinalPos = new THREE.Vector3();
  }

  setHouse(house) {
    this.house = house;
  }

  setAspect(aspectKey) {
    const spec = CONFIG.aspectModes[aspectKey];
    if (!spec) return spec;
    this.aspectKey = aspectKey;
    this.camera.aspect = spec.aspect;
    this.camera.fov = spec.fov;
    this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    this.viewmodelCamera.aspect = spec.aspect;
    this.viewmodelCamera.fov = spec.fov;
    this.viewmodelCamera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    // Adjust held viewmodel slightly for narrower 9:16 frame
    if (aspectKey === "9:16") {
      this.heldItemMount.position.set(0.18, -0.24, -0.5);
    } else {
      this.heldItemMount.position.set(0.28, -0.22, -0.5);
    }
    return spec;
  }

  hasHeldProp() {
    return Boolean(this.heldPropMesh);
  }

  setInspectionMode(isOrbit) {
    this.mode = isOrbit ? "orbit" : "pov";
    this.orbitControls.enabled = isOrbit;
    this.heldItemMount.visible = !isOrbit;
    if (isOrbit) {
      if (this.camera.position.y < 3.0) {
        this.camera.position.set(0, 9.5, 11.5);
        this.orbitControls.target.set(0, 0.5, 0.0);
      }
      this.orbitControls.update();
    }
  }

  reset(spawnPos = null, lookAtPos = null) {
    if (this.activeWalk?._resolve) this.activeWalk._resolve();
    if (this._lookResolve) {
      this._lookResolve();
      this._lookResolve = null;
    }
    if (this._crouchResolve) {
      this._crouchResolve();
      this._crouchResolve = null;
    }
    if (this._fadeResolve) {
      this._fadeResolve();
      this._fadeResolve = null;
    }
    if (this.activeGesture?._resolve) this.activeGesture._resolve();

    this.activeWalk = null;
    this.isWalking = false;
    this.bobPhase = 0;
    this.bobAmount = 0;
    this.activeGesture = null;
    this.shakeIntensity = 0;
    this.quakeTimer = 0;
    this.trackingRef = null;

    this.baseEyeHeight = PLAYER_EYE_HEIGHT;
    this.crouchStartHeight = PLAYER_EYE_HEIGHT;
    this.crouchTargetHeight = PLAYER_EYE_HEIGHT;
    this.crouchElapsed = 1;

    this.fadeAlpha = 0;
    this.fadeStart = 0;
    this.fadeTarget = 0;
    this.fadeElapsed = 1;

    this.holdProp(null);

    if (spawnPos) {
      this.playerPos.set(spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y || 0, spawnPos.z);
    } else {
      this.playerPos.set(0, 0, -2.6);
    }

    if (lookAtPos) {
      const { yaw, pitch } = this._computeYawPitchTo(lookAtPos);
      this.yaw = yaw;
      this.pitch = pitch;
    } else {
      this.yaw = 0;
      this.pitch = -0.04;
    }
    this.lookStartYaw = this.yaw;
    this.lookTargetYaw = this.yaw;
    this.lookStartPitch = this.pitch;
    this.lookTargetPitch = this.pitch;
    this.lookElapsed = 1;
  }

  _resolveTargetPosition(ref, resolver, out) {
    if (!ref || !resolver) return false;
    if (typeof ref === "object" && "x" in ref && "z" in ref) {
      out.set(ref.x, ref.y ?? 1.35, ref.z);
      return true;
    }
    const actor = resolver.getActor(ref);
    if (actor) {
      actor.getHeadWorldPosition(out);
      out.y -= 0.08;
      return true;
    }
    const prop = resolver.getProp ? resolver.getProp(ref) : null;
    if (prop) {
      out.copy(prop.mesh.position);
      out.y += 0.08;
      return true;
    }
    const wp = resolver.getWaypoint(ref);
    if (wp) {
      // Surface waypoints (e.g. counter top y=1.13) target just above the surface rather than adding +1.35m eye height.
      const yOff = (wp.y || 0) > 0.2 ? 0.12 : 1.35;
      out.set(wp.x, (wp.y || 0) + yOff, wp.z);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  _computeYawPitchTo(targetPos) {
    const eyeY = this.playerPos.y + this.baseEyeHeight;
    const dx = targetPos.x - this.playerPos.x;
    const dy = targetPos.y - eyeY;
    const dz = targetPos.z - this.playerPos.z;
    const horizDist = Math.max(1e-4, Math.hypot(dx, dz));
    // Three.js camera looks down -Z; yaw = atan2(-dx, -dz)
    const yaw = Math.atan2(-dx, -dz);
    const pitch = clamp(Math.atan2(dy, horizDist), MIN_CAMERA_PITCH, MAX_CAMERA_PITCH);
    return { yaw, pitch };
  }

  _sampleAlongWalk(walk, dist) {
    const out = new THREE.Vector3();
    if (!walk || !walk.points || walk.points.length === 0) return out;
    if (dist <= 0 || walk.totalLength < 1e-4) return out.copy(walk.points[0]);
    if (dist >= walk.totalLength) return out.copy(walk.points[walk.points.length - 1]);

    let seg = 0;
    while (seg < walk.cumDist.length - 2 && walk.cumDist[seg + 1] < dist) {
      seg += 1;
    }
    const segStart = walk.cumDist[seg];
    const segLen = Math.max(1e-5, walk.cumDist[seg + 1] - segStart);
    const alpha = clamp((dist - segStart) / segLen, 0, 1);
    return out.lerpVectors(walk.points[seg], walk.points[seg + 1], alpha);
  }

  startWalk({
    points,
    duration = null,
    speed = null,
    autoLook = true,
    segments = null,
    maxVerticalSpeed = 1.55,
  }) {
    if (this.activeWalk?._resolve) {
      this.activeWalk._resolve();
    }
    if (!points || points.length < 2) return Promise.resolve();

    const walkSpeed = Math.max(0.4, Number(speed) || BASE_WALK_SPEED);
    const cumDist = [0];
    let totalLength = 0;
    let computedDuration = 0;
    const segMeta = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i += 1) {
      const a = points[i - 1];
      const b = points[i];
      const segLen = a.distanceTo(b);
      const dy = Math.abs(b.y - a.y);
      const meta = segments?.[i - 1] || {};
      const isJumpArc = Boolean(meta.isJumpArc);
      const isStair = !isJumpArc && dy > 0.15;

      let segDur = 0;
      if (isJumpArc) {
        segDur = Math.max(0.55, segLen / Math.max(walkSpeed, BASE_JUMP_SPEED));
      } else if (isStair) {
        const stairSpd = Math.min(walkSpeed, BASE_STAIR_SPEED);
        segDur = Math.max(segLen / stairSpd, dy / maxVerticalSpeed);
      } else {
        segDur = segLen / walkSpeed;
      }

      const rawArc = meta.arcHeight || 0.44;
      const safeArc = Math.max(a.y, b.y) < 1.5 ? Math.min(rawArc, 0.18) : rawArc;

      computedDuration += segDur;
      segMeta.push({
        isJumpArc,
        isStair,
        arcHeight: safeArc,
        startY: a.y,
        endY: b.y,
      });
      totalLength += segLen;
      cumDist.push(totalLength);
    }

    // Dynamic distance-based timing: never teleport across long routes on short fixed durations.
    const requestedMin = Number(duration) > 0 ? Number(duration) : 0;
    const effectiveDuration = Math.max(0.18, computedDuration, requestedMin);

    let resolveWalk = null;
    const donePromise = new Promise((resolve) => {
      resolveWalk = resolve;
    });

    this.activeWalk = {
      points,
      cumDist,
      segMeta,
      totalLength,
      elapsed: 0,
      duration: effectiveDuration,
      maxVerticalSpeed,
      autoLook,
      _resolve: resolveWalk,
    };
    this.isWalking = true;

    if (autoLook && totalLength > 0.08) {
      const lookAhead = this._sampleAlongWalk(this.activeWalk, Math.min(totalLength, 1.25));
      lookAhead.y = this.playerPos.y + this.baseEyeHeight - 0.05;
      this.lookAtPosition(lookAhead, { duration: 0.35, trackRef: null });
    }
    return donePromise;
  }

  lookAtPosition(targetPos, { duration = 0.25, trackRef = null } = {}) {
    if (this._lookResolve) {
      this._lookResolve();
      this._lookResolve = null;
    }
    const { yaw, pitch } = this._computeYawPitchTo(targetPos);
    this.lookStartYaw = this.yaw;
    this.lookStartPitch = this.pitch;
    this.lookTargetYaw = this.yaw + wrapAngleDiff(yaw, this.yaw);
    this.lookTargetPitch = pitch;
    this.lookElapsed = 0;
    this.lookDuration = Math.max(0.01, duration);
    this.trackingRef = trackRef;
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this._lookResolve = resolve;
    });
  }

  lookAt(ref, { duration = 0.25, track = false } = {}, resolver) {
    if (this._resolveTargetPosition(ref, resolver, this._tmpTargetPos)) {
      return this.lookAtPosition(this._tmpTargetPos, {
        duration,
        trackRef: track ? ref : null,
      });
    }
    return Promise.resolve();
  }

  crouch({ crouched = true, duration = 0.35 } = {}) {
    if (this._crouchResolve) {
      this._crouchResolve();
      this._crouchResolve = null;
    }
    this.crouchStartHeight = this.baseEyeHeight;
    this.crouchTargetHeight = crouched
      ? CONFIG.playerCrouchHeight
      : PLAYER_EYE_HEIGHT;
    this.crouchElapsed = 0;
    this.crouchDuration = Math.max(0.05, duration);
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this._crouchResolve = resolve;
    });
  }

  triggerGesture(type = "nod_yes", duration = 0.7) {
    if (type === "shake_panic") {
      this.shakeIntensity = Math.max(this.shakeIntensity, 0.4);
      return Promise.resolve();
    }
    if (type === "shake_earthquake") {
      this.quakeTimer = Math.max(this.quakeTimer, Math.max(0.5, duration));
      this.shakeIntensity = Math.max(this.shakeIntensity, 0.72);
      return Promise.resolve();
    }
    if (this.activeGesture?._resolve) {
      this.activeGesture._resolve();
    }
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this.activeGesture = {
        type,
        elapsed: 0,
        duration: Math.max(0.2, duration),
        _resolve: resolve,
      };
    });
  }

  holdProp(propType = null) {
    if (this.heldPropMesh) {
      this.heldItemMount.remove(this.heldPropMesh);
      this.heldPropMesh.traverse((n) => {
        if (n.geometry) n.geometry.dispose();
        if (n.material) {
          const ms = Array.isArray(n.material) ? n.material : [n.material];
          for (const m of ms) {
            if (m.map) m.map.dispose();
            m.dispose();
          }
        }
      });
      this.heldPropMesh = null;
    }

    this.heldPropType = propType || null;
    if (!propType) return;

    const mesh = createPropMesh(propType);
    // Scale cleanly for crisp FPS bottom-right framing
    mesh.scale.setScalar(propType === "emoji_ball" ? 0.45 : 0.72);
    if (propType === "golden_key") {
      mesh.rotation.set(0.3, -0.4, -0.35);
    } else if (propType === "toy_blaster") {
      mesh.rotation.set(0.05, -0.15, 0);
    } else if (propType === "red_button") {
      mesh.rotation.set(0.35, -0.25, 0);
    }
    this.heldItemMount.add(mesh);
    this.heldPropMesh = mesh;
    this.heldEquipPop = 0;
  }

  useHeldProp() {
    this.heldUseTimer = 0.32;
  }

  startFade({ to = 1, duration = 0.4 } = {}) {
    if (this._fadeResolve) {
      this._fadeResolve();
      this._fadeResolve = null;
    }
    this.fadeStart = this.fadeAlpha;
    this.fadeTarget = clamp(to, 0, 1);
    this.fadeElapsed = 0;
    this.fadeDuration = Math.max(0.02, duration);
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this._fadeResolve = resolve;
    });
  }

  getEyeWorldPosition(out = new THREE.Vector3()) {
    return out.set(
      this.playerPos.x,
      this.playerPos.y + this.baseEyeHeight,
      this.playerPos.z
    );
  }

  update(dt, resolver) {
    // Update fade even if in POV
    if (this.fadeElapsed < this.fadeDuration) {
      this.fadeElapsed = Math.min(this.fadeDuration, this.fadeElapsed + dt);
      const ft = easeInOutSine(this.fadeElapsed / this.fadeDuration);
      this.fadeAlpha = this.fadeStart + (this.fadeTarget - this.fadeStart) * ft;
      if (this.fadeElapsed >= this.fadeDuration && this._fadeResolve) {
        const res = this._fadeResolve;
        this._fadeResolve = null;
        res();
      }
    }

    if (this.mode === "orbit") {
      this.orbitControls.update();
      return;
    }

    // Crouch interpolation
    if (this.crouchElapsed < this.crouchDuration) {
      this.crouchElapsed = Math.min(this.crouchDuration, this.crouchElapsed + dt);
      const ct = easeInOutSine(this.crouchElapsed / this.crouchDuration);
      this.baseEyeHeight =
        this.crouchStartHeight + (this.crouchTargetHeight - this.crouchStartHeight) * ct;
      if (this.crouchElapsed >= this.crouchDuration && this._crouchResolve) {
        const res = this._crouchResolve;
        this._crouchResolve = null;
        res();
      }
    }

    // Walking along polyline
    if (this.activeWalk) {
      const prevY = this.playerPos.y;
      this.activeWalk.elapsed += dt;
      const rawT = clamp(this.activeWalk.elapsed / this.activeWalk.duration, 0, 1);
      const t = rawT * 0.9 + easeInOutSine(rawT) * 0.1;
      const targetDist = t * this.activeWalk.totalLength;

      if (this.activeWalk.totalLength < 1e-4 || this.activeWalk.points.length < 2) {
        this.playerPos.copy(this.activeWalk.points[this.activeWalk.points.length - 1]);
      } else {
        let seg = 0;
        while (
          seg < this.activeWalk.cumDist.length - 2 &&
          this.activeWalk.cumDist[seg + 1] < targetDist
        ) {
          seg += 1;
        }
        const segStart = this.activeWalk.cumDist[seg];
        const segLen = Math.max(1e-5, this.activeWalk.cumDist[seg + 1] - segStart);
        const alpha = clamp((targetDist - segStart) / segLen, 0, 1);
        const pA = this.activeWalk.points[seg];
        const pB = this.activeWalk.points[seg + 1];
        const meta = this.activeWalk.segMeta?.[seg] || {};

        this.playerPos.lerpVectors(pA, pB, alpha);

        if (meta.isJumpArc) {
          // Smooth parabolic jump onto/off elevated furniture
          this.playerPos.y += Math.sin(alpha * Math.PI) * (meta.arcHeight || 0.44);
        } else if (meta.isStair) {
          // Staircase traversal: smoothly clamp vertical speed to staircase slope
          const maxStepY = (this.activeWalk.maxVerticalSpeed || 1.55) * Math.max(dt, 1 / 120) * 1.35;
          const dyStep = this.playerPos.y - prevY;
          if (Math.abs(dyStep) > maxStepY) {
            this.playerPos.y = prevY + Math.sign(dyStep) * maxStepY;
          }
        } else {
          // Strict flat floor lock: keep player Y locked to floor node.y (eye = node.y + 1.55)
          this.playerPos.y = Math.abs(pB.y - pA.y) < 0.05 ? pA.y : pA.y + (pB.y - pA.y) * alpha;
        }

        if (this.activeWalk.autoLook && !this.trackingRef && this.lookElapsed >= this.lookDuration) {
          const lookDist = Math.min(this.activeWalk.totalLength, targetDist + 1.15);
          const ahead = this._sampleAlongWalk(this.activeWalk, lookDist);
          const dx = ahead.x - this.playerPos.x;
          const dz = ahead.z - this.playerPos.z;
          const horiz = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
          if (horiz > 0.12) {
            const desiredYaw = Math.atan2(-dx, -dz);
            this.yaw += wrapAngleDiff(desiredYaw, this.yaw) * Math.min(1, dt * 5.2);
          }
          const desiredPitch = meta.isStair
            ? clamp(Math.atan2(pB.y - pA.y, Math.max(0.4, Math.hypot(pB.x - pA.x, pB.z - pA.z))) * 0.42, -0.26, 0.22)
            : -0.04;
          this.pitch += (desiredPitch - this.pitch) * Math.min(1, dt * 4.5);
        }
      }

      this.bobPhase += dt * 11.2;
      this.bobAmount += (1 - this.bobAmount) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);

      if (rawT >= 1) {
        const finished = this.activeWalk;
        this.playerPos.copy(finished.points[finished.points.length - 1]);
        this.activeWalk = null;
        this.isWalking = false;
        if (finished._resolve) finished._resolve();
      }
    } else {
      this.bobAmount *= Math.exp(-10 * dt);
    }

    // Look tracking / whip-pan
    if (this.trackingRef && this._resolveTargetPosition(this.trackingRef, resolver, this._tmpTargetPos)) {
      const { yaw, pitch } = this._computeYawPitchTo(this._tmpTargetPos);
      if (this.lookElapsed < this.lookDuration) {
        this.lookTargetYaw = this.lookStartYaw + wrapAngleDiff(yaw, this.lookStartYaw);
        this.lookTargetPitch = pitch;
      } else {
        this.yaw += wrapAngleDiff(yaw, this.yaw) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);
        this.pitch += (pitch - this.pitch) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);
      }
    }

    if (this.lookElapsed < this.lookDuration) {
      this.lookElapsed = Math.min(this.lookDuration, this.lookElapsed + dt);
      const lt = easeOutCubic(this.lookElapsed / this.lookDuration);
      this.yaw = this.lookStartYaw + (this.lookTargetYaw - this.lookStartYaw) * lt;
      this.pitch = this.lookStartPitch + (this.lookTargetPitch - this.lookStartPitch) * lt;
      if (this.lookElapsed >= this.lookDuration && this._lookResolve) {
        const res = this._lookResolve;
        this._lookResolve = null;
        res();
      }
    }

    // Gestures
    let gesturePitch = 0;
    let gestureYaw = 0;
    let gestureRoll = 0;
    let gestureLeanX = 0;
    if (this.activeGesture) {
      this.activeGesture.elapsed += dt;
      const gt = clamp(this.activeGesture.elapsed / this.activeGesture.duration, 0, 1);
      const envelope = Math.sin(gt * Math.PI);
      if (this.activeGesture.type === "nod_yes") {
        gesturePitch = Math.sin(gt * Math.PI * 4) * 0.18 * envelope;
      } else if (this.activeGesture.type === "shake_no") {
        gestureYaw = Math.sin(gt * Math.PI * 4) * 0.24 * envelope;
      } else if (this.activeGesture.type === "peek_lean") {
        gestureLeanX = Math.sin(gt * Math.PI) * 0.32;
        gestureRoll = -Math.sin(gt * Math.PI) * 0.12;
      }
      if (gt >= 1) {
        const doneGesture = this.activeGesture;
        this.activeGesture = null;
        if (doneGesture._resolve) doneGesture._resolve();
      }
    }

    // Subtle footsteps head-bob + damped handheld shake
    const bobY = Math.sin(this.bobPhase) * 0.028 * this.bobAmount;
    const bobX = Math.cos(this.bobPhase * 0.5) * 0.014 * this.bobAmount;

    let shakeX = 0;
    let shakeY = 0;
    let shakeRoll = 0;
    if (this.quakeTimer > 0) {
      this.quakeTimer = Math.max(0, this.quakeTimer - dt);
      this.shakeIntensity = Math.max(this.shakeIntensity, 0.72);
    }
    if (this.shakeIntensity > 0.002) {
      this.shakePhase += dt * 28;
      shakeX = Math.sin(this.shakePhase * 1.1) * this.shakeIntensity * 0.028;
      shakeY = Math.cos(this.shakePhase * 1.45) * this.shakeIntensity * 0.028;
      shakeRoll = Math.sin(this.shakePhase * 0.9) * this.shakeIntensity * 0.022;
      if (this.quakeTimer <= 0) {
        this.shakeIntensity *= Math.exp(-7.5 * dt);
      }
    } else {
      this.shakeIntensity = 0;
    }

    const finalYaw = this.yaw + gestureYaw;
    const finalPitch = clamp(this.pitch + gesturePitch, MIN_CAMERA_PITCH, MAX_CAMERA_PITCH);
    const rightX = Math.cos(finalYaw);
    const rightZ = -Math.sin(finalYaw);

    this._tmpEyePos.set(
      this.playerPos.x,
      this.playerPos.y + this.baseEyeHeight,
      this.playerPos.z
    );
    this._tmpFinalPos.set(
      this._tmpEyePos.x + rightX * (bobX + gestureLeanX + shakeX),
      this._tmpEyePos.y + bobY + shakeY,
      this._tmpEyePos.z + rightZ * (bobX + gestureLeanX + shakeX)
    );

    if (this.house && typeof this.house.clampCameraPosition === "function") {
      this.camera.position.copy(
        this.house.clampCameraPosition(this._tmpEyePos, this._tmpFinalPos, 0.05)
      );
    } else {
      this.camera.position.copy(this._tmpFinalPos);
    }

    this.camera.rotation.set(finalPitch, finalYaw, gestureRoll + shakeRoll, "YXZ");

    // Animate bottom-right FPS held prop (deterministic viewmodel pass)
    if (this.heldPropMesh) {
      this.vmBobTime += dt;
      this.heldEquipPop = Math.min(1, this.heldEquipPop + dt * 6);
      const popScale = easeOutCubic(this.heldEquipPop);
      const baseScale = this.heldPropType === "emoji_ball" ? 0.36 : 0.72;
      this.heldPropMesh.scale.setScalar(baseScale * popScale);

      const baseX = this.aspectKey === "9:16" ? 0.18 : 0.28;
      const baseY = this.aspectKey === "9:16" ? -0.24 : -0.22;
      let pokeZ = 0;
      let pokePitch = 0;
      if (this.heldUseTimer > 0) {
        this.heldUseTimer = Math.max(0, this.heldUseTimer - dt);
        const u = Math.sin((this.heldUseTimer / 0.32) * Math.PI);
        pokeZ = -u * 0.14;
        pokePitch = -u * 0.45;
      }
      this.heldItemMount.position.set(
        baseX + Math.cos(this.bobPhase * 0.5) * 0.012 * this.bobAmount,
        baseY + Math.sin(this.bobPhase) * 0.016 * this.bobAmount + Math.sin(this.vmBobTime * 3.0) * 0.005,
        -0.5 + pokeZ
      );
      this.heldItemMount.rotation.set(pokePitch, 0, 0);
    }
  }

  dispose() {
    this.holdProp(null);
    this.orbitControls.dispose();
  }
}


================================================================
===== FILE: src/sfxEngine.js =====
================================================================
import { CONFIG, SFX_MANIFEST } from "./config.js";

/**
 * Web Audio API Mixer with:
 * - Preloaded AudioBuffers from ./SoundEffects
 * - Looping BGM + comedic dead-silence "cutBgm" ducking
 * - Movement-synced footsteps (player + characters)
 * - Context-matched 1-2s Cartoon SFX Pacer (whip-pans, bubble pops, pickups, reactions)
 * - Dual routing to live speakers + MediaStreamAudioDestinationNode
 * - Offline event & BGM capture for GitHub Actions FFmpeg export
 */
export class SfxEngine {
  constructor() {
    this.ctx = null;
    this.masterGain = null;
    this.bgmGain = null;
    this.compressor = null;
    this.streamDest = null;

    this.buffers = new Map();
    this.activeSources = new Set();
    this.lastPlayedByKey = new Map();

    // BGM state
    this.bgmSource = null;
    this.bgmKey = null;
    this.bgmVolume = 0.28;
    this.bgmDuckTimer = 0;

    this.pacerEnabled = CONFIG.sfxPacer.enabledByDefault;
    this.muted = false;
    this.lastAnySfxTime = -Infinity;
    this.stepAccumulator = 0;
    this.nextGapThreshold = 1.65;

    // Offline deterministic capture for FFmpeg export
    this.exportMode = false;
    this.recordedEvents = [];
    this.recordedBgm = []; // { startTime, endTime, path, volume }
    this.recordedDucks = []; // { startTime, endTime }
    this._activeExportBgm = null;
  }

  ensureContext() {
    if (this.ctx) return this.ctx;

    const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    this.ctx = new AudioCtx();

    this.compressor = this.ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
    this.compressor.threshold.value = -10;
    this.compressor.knee.value = 12;
    this.compressor.ratio.value = 6;
    this.compressor.attack.value = 0.004;
    this.compressor.release.value = 0.14;

    this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
    this.masterGain.gain.value = this.muted ? 0 : CONFIG.sfxPacer.masterVolume;

    this.bgmGain = this.ctx.createGain();
    this.bgmGain.gain.value = this.bgmVolume;
    this.bgmGain.connect(this.compressor);

    this.streamDest = this.ctx.createMediaStreamDestination();

    this.compressor.connect(this.masterGain);
    this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
    this.masterGain.connect(this.streamDest);

    return this.ctx;
  }

  async resume() {
    const ctx = this.ensureContext();
    if (ctx.state === "suspended") {
      await ctx.resume();
    }
  }

  async preloadAll(onProgress = null) {
    const ctx = this.ensureContext();
    const entries = Object.entries(SFX_MANIFEST);
    let completed = 0;

    await Promise.all(
      entries.map(async ([key, meta]) => {
        try {
          const response = await fetch(encodeURI(meta.path));
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
          const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
          const audioBuffer = await ctx.decodeAudioData(arrayBuffer);
          this.buffers.set(key, audioBuffer);
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn(`[SfxEngine] Could not load SFX "${key}":`, err);
        } finally {
          completed += 1;
          if (onProgress) onProgress(completed, entries.length, key);
        }
      })
    );
  }

  getMediaStream() {
    this.ensureContext();
    return this.streamDest.stream;
  }

  setMuted(muted) {
    this.muted = Boolean(muted);
    if (this.masterGain && this.ctx) {
      this.masterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(
        this.muted ? 0 : CONFIG.sfxPacer.masterVolume,
        this.ctx.currentTime,
        0.02
      );
    }
  }

  setPacerEnabled(enabled) {
    this.pacerEnabled = Boolean(enabled);
  }

  resetPacerClock(simTime = 0) {
    this.lastAnySfxTime = simTime - 0.4;
    this.stepAccumulator = 0;
    this.nextGapThreshold = 1.65;
    this.bgmDuckTimer = 0;
  }

  startEventCapture() {
    this.exportMode = true;
    this.recordedEvents = [];
    this.recordedBgm = [];
    this.recordedDucks = [];
    this._activeExportBgm = null;
    this.lastPlayedByKey.clear();
  }

  stopEventCapture(finalTime = null) {
    if (this._activeExportBgm && finalTime !== null) {
      this._activeExportBgm.endTime = Math.max(
        this._activeExportBgm.startTime,
        finalTime
      );
      this.recordedBgm.push(this._activeExportBgm);
      this._activeExportBgm = null;
    }
    this.exportMode = false;
    return {
      sfx: [...this.recordedEvents],
      bgm: [...this.recordedBgm],
      ducks: [...this.recordedDucks],
    };
  }

  playBgm(key, { volume = 0.25, simTime = 0 } = {}) {
    const meta = SFX_MANIFEST[key];
    if (!meta) return;

    if (this.exportMode) {
      if (this._activeExportBgm) {
        this._activeExportBgm.endTime = Math.max(
          this._activeExportBgm.startTime,
          simTime
        );
        this.recordedBgm.push(this._activeExportBgm);
      }
      this._activeExportBgm = {
        key,
        path: meta.path,
        volume,
        startTime: Math.max(0, simTime),
        endTime: null,
      };
      return;
    }

    this.stopBgm();
    const buffer = this.buffers.get(key);
    if (!buffer) return;
    const ctx = this.ensureContext();
    if (ctx.state !== "running") return;

    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buffer;
    src.loop = true;
    this.bgmVolume = volume;
    this.bgmGain.gain.setValueAtTime(volume, ctx.currentTime);
    src.connect(this.bgmGain);
    src.start(ctx.currentTime);
    this.bgmSource = src;
    this.bgmKey = key;
  }

  stopBgm(simTime = 0) {
    if (this.exportMode && this._activeExportBgm) {
      this._activeExportBgm.endTime = Math.max(
        this._activeExportBgm.startTime,
        simTime
      );
      this.recordedBgm.push(this._activeExportBgm);
      this._activeExportBgm = null;
    }
    if (this.bgmSource) {
      try {
        this.bgmSource.stop();
      } catch {
        // Ignore already stopped
      }
      this.bgmSource = null;
      this.bgmKey = null;
    }
  }

  muteBgmFor(durationSec = 1.2, simTime = 0) {
    this.bgmDuckTimer = Math.max(this.bgmDuckTimer, durationSec);
    if (this.exportMode) {
      this.recordedDucks.push({
        startTime: Math.max(0, simTime),
        endTime: Math.max(0, simTime + durationSec),
      });
      return;
    }
    if (this.bgmGain && this.ctx) {
      this.bgmGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime);
    }
  }

  stopAll() {
    this.stopBgm();
    for (const src of this.activeSources) {
      try {
        src.stop();
      } catch {
        // Ignore
      }
    }
    this.activeSources.clear();
  }

  play(
    key,
    {
      volume = null,
      pitch = 1.0,
      pitchJitter = 0.08,
      simTime = null,
      cooldown = CONFIG.sfxPacer.globalCooldownSec,
      maxDuration = null,
      cutBgm = false,
      cutBgmDuration = 1.4,
    } = {}
  ) {
    const meta = SFX_MANIFEST[key];
    if (!meta) return false;

    const randomJitter =
      pitchJitter > 0 ? (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * pitchJitter : 0;
    const effectivePitch = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2.0, pitch + randomJitter));
    const targetVol = volume ?? meta.defaultVolume ?? 0.7;

    if (cutBgm) {
      this.muteBgmFor(cutBgmDuration, simTime ?? 0);
    }

    if (this.exportMode) {
      const clockTime = simTime ?? 0;
      const prevKeyTime = this.lastPlayedByKey.get(key) ?? -Infinity;
      if (clockTime - prevKeyTime < cooldown) {
        return false;
      }
      this.lastPlayedByKey.set(key, clockTime);
      if (simTime !== null) {
        this.lastAnySfxTime = simTime;
      }
      this.recordedEvents.push({
        time: Math.max(0, clockTime),
        key,
        path: meta.path,
        volume: targetVol,
        pitch: effectivePitch,
        maxDuration,
      });
      return true;
    }

    const buffer = this.buffers.get(key);
    if (!buffer) return false;

    const ctx = this.ensureContext();
    if (ctx.state !== "running") return false;

    const nowWall = ctx.currentTime;
    const prevKeyTime = this.lastPlayedByKey.get(key) ?? -Infinity;
    if (nowWall - prevKeyTime < cooldown) {
      return false;
    }

    const source = ctx.createBufferSource();
    source.buffer = buffer;
    source.playbackRate.value = effectivePitch;

    const gainNode = ctx.createGain();
    gainNode.gain.value = targetVol;

    source.connect(gainNode);
    gainNode.connect(this.compressor);

    this.activeSources.add(source);
    this.lastPlayedByKey.set(key, nowWall);
    if (simTime !== null) {
      this.lastAnySfxTime = simTime;
    }

    source.onended = () => {
      this.activeSources.delete(source);
      gainNode.disconnect();
    };

    if (maxDuration && maxDuration > 0 && buffer.duration > maxDuration) {
      source.start(nowWall, 0, maxDuration);
    } else {
      source.start(nowWall);
    }
    return true;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Kids-Comedy POV Foley & 1-2s Slapstick Pacer Hooks
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  notifyWhipPan(duration, simTime) {
    if (!this.pacerEnabled || duration > 0.25) return;
    if (simTime - this.lastAnySfxTime < 0.35) return;
    this.play("whoosh", {
      volume: 0.55,
      pitch: 1.05,
      pitchJitter: 0.08,
      simTime,
      maxDuration: 0.45,
    });
  }

  notifyBubblePop(iconName, simTime) {
    if (!this.pacerEnabled) return;
    if (simTime - this.lastAnySfxTime < 0.25) return;
    const key =
      iconName === "exclamation" || iconName === "angry" ? "discord" : "pop";
    this.play(key, {
      volume: 0.72,
      pitchJitter: 0.08,
      simTime,
      maxDuration: 0.65,
    });
  }

  notifyPickup(propName, simTime) {
    if (!this.pacerEnabled || !propName) return;
    this.play("kaching", {
      volume: 0.68,
      pitchJitter: 0.06,
      simTime,
      maxDuration: 0.9,
    });
  }

  notifyAction(animName, simTime) {
    if (!this.pacerEnabled) return;
    if (simTime - this.lastAnySfxTime < 0.35) return;
    const map = {
      jump: { key: "whoosh", volume: 0.5, pitch: 1.08, maxDuration: 0.55 },
      punch: { key: "bonk", volume: 0.78, pitch: 1.0, maxDuration: 0.85 },
      laugh: { key: "granny_laugh", volume: 0.72, pitch: 1.0, maxDuration: 1.35 },
      terrified: { key: "glitch", volume: 0.55, pitch: 1.05, maxDuration: 0.8 },
      panic: { key: "glitch", volume: 0.62, pitch: 1.12, maxDuration: 0.85 },
    };
    const spec = map[animName];
    if (spec) {
      this.play(spec.key, {
        volume: spec.volume,
        pitch: spec.pitch,
        pitchJitter: 0.06,
        simTime,
        cooldown: 0.35,
        maxDuration: spec.maxDuration,
      });
    }
  }

  tickPacer(simDt, simTime, { anyMoving = false, moveAnim = "walk" } = {}) {
    // Restore ducked BGM smoothly after comedy silence cut
    if (!this.exportMode && this.bgmDuckTimer > 0) {
      this.bgmDuckTimer = Math.max(0, this.bgmDuckTimer - simDt);
      if (this.bgmDuckTimer === 0 && this.bgmGain && this.ctx) {
        this.bgmGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(this.bgmVolume, this.ctx.currentTime, 0.08);
      }
    }

    if (!this.pacerEnabled) return;

    if (anyMoving) {
      const isRun = moveAnim === "run" || moveAnim === "run2";
      const isSneak = moveAnim === "sneak";
      const stepPeriod = isRun
        ? CONFIG.sfxPacer.footstepIntervalRun
        : isSneak
          ? CONFIG.sfxPacer.footstepIntervalSneak
          : CONFIG.sfxPacer.footstepIntervalWalk;

      this.stepAccumulator += simDt;
      if (this.stepAccumulator >= stepPeriod) {
        this.stepAccumulator -= stepPeriod;
        this.play("squidward_walk", {
          volume: isRun ? 0.48 : isSneak ? 0.34 : 0.42,
          pitch: isRun ? 1.26 : isSneak ? 0.82 : 0.98,
          pitchJitter: 0.06,
          simTime,
          cooldown: 0.2,
          maxDuration: isRun ? 0.22 : 0.34,
        });
      }
      return;
    }

    this.stepAccumulator = 0;

    // Gentle comedic gap pacer only if idle for > 1.7s
    const gap = simTime - this.lastAnySfxTime;
    if (gap >= this.nextGapThreshold) {
      const cues = ["pop", "ding", "click"];
      const pick = cues[Math.floor(Math.random() * cues.length)];
      if (
        this.play(pick, {
          volume: 0.35,
          pitchJitter: 0.1,
          simTime,
          maxDuration: 0.45,
        })
      ) {
        this.nextGapThreshold =
          CONFIG.sfxPacer.minIntervalSec +
          Math.random() *
            (CONFIG.sfxPacer.maxIntervalSec - CONFIG.sfxPacer.minIntervalSec);
      }
    }
  }
}


================================================================
===== FILE: src/studioUI.js =====
================================================================
import { CONFIG, EPISODE_PRESETS, SET_MAP, SFX_MANIFEST } from "./config.js";

/**
 * Interactive Studio UI controller:
 * - Transport bar + Aspect (16:9 / 9:16) + POV / Orbit toggle
 * - Character & Player POV test controls (animations, bubbles, held props, gestures)
 * - 25-clip Soundboard
 * - Waypoint Calibrator (click-to-teleport + live coordinate readout)
 * - Live Multi-Skit JSON Editor
 * - Real-time WebM export (MediaRecorder) + Fade-to-black overlay
 */
export class StudioUI {
  constructor({
    runner,
    sfxEngine,
    playerCamera,
    cameraDirector,
    characters,
    renderer,
    onLoadEpisode,
    onAspectChange,
    onExportStateChange,
  }) {
    this.runner = runner;
    this.sfxEngine = sfxEngine;
    this.playerCamera = playerCamera;
    this.cameraDirector = cameraDirector;
    this.characters = characters;
    this.renderer = renderer;
    this.onLoadEpisode = onLoadEpisode;
    this.onAspectChange = onAspectChange;
    this.onExportStateChange = onExportStateChange;

    this.isRecording = false;
    this.activeTab = "director";

    this.els = {
      presetSelect: document.getElementById("presetSelect"),
      playPauseBtn: document.getElementById("playPauseBtn"),
      stopBtn: document.getElementById("stopBtn"),
      timelineSlider: document.getElementById("timelineSlider"),
      timeReadout: document.getElementById("timeReadout"),
      aspectToggleBtn: document.getElementById("aspectToggleBtn"),
      camModeBtn: document.getElementById("camModeBtn"),
      pacerToggleBtn: document.getElementById("pacerToggleBtn"),
      muteBtn: document.getElementById("muteBtn"),
      exportBtn: document.getElementById("exportBtn"),
      panelToggleBtn: document.getElementById("panelToggleBtn"),
      studioPanel: document.getElementById("studioPanel"),
      episodeEditor: document.getElementById("episodeEditor"),
      applyJsonBtn: document.getElementById("applyJsonBtn"),
      soundboardGrid: document.getElementById("soundboardGrid"),
      waypointList: document.getElementById("waypointList"),
      coordReadout: document.getElementById("coordReadout"),
      copyCoordBtn: document.getElementById("copyCoordBtn"),
      statusToast: document.getElementById("statusToast"),
      fadeOverlay: document.getElementById("fadeOverlay"),
      stageContainer: document.getElementById("stageContainer"),
    };

    this.bindEvents();
    this.populatePresets();
    this.populateSoundboard();
    this.populateWaypoints();
    this.syncButtons();
  }

  bindEvents() {
    this.els.playPauseBtn?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      await this.sfxEngine.resume();
      if (this.runner.isPlaying) {
        this.runner.pause();
      } else {
        this.runner.play();
      }
      this.syncButtons();
    });

    this.els.stopBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      this.runner.stop();
      this.syncButtons();
    });

    this.els.timelineSlider?.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
      const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);
      this.runner.seek(val);
    });

    this.els.presetSelect?.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
      const key = e.target.value;
      const preset = EPISODE_PRESETS[key];
      if (!preset) return;
      this.els.episodeEditor.value = JSON.stringify(preset, null, 2);
      this.onLoadEpisode(preset);
      this.toast(`Loaded "${preset.title}"`);
    });

    this.els.aspectToggleBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const next =
        this.playerCamera.aspectKey === "16:9" ? "9:16" : "16:9";
      this.playerCamera.setAspect(next);
      this.onAspectChange?.(next);
      this.syncButtons();
      this.toast(`Aspect switched to ${next}`);
    });

    this.els.camModeBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const nextIsOrbit = this.playerCamera.mode !== "orbit";
      this.playerCamera.setInspectionMode(nextIsOrbit);
      this.syncButtons();
      this.toast(
        nextIsOrbit
          ? "Orbit Calibrator (Drag + Scroll)"
          : "POV Camera (First-Person)"
      );
    });

    this.els.pacerToggleBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      this.sfxEngine.setPacerEnabled(!this.sfxEngine.pacerEnabled);
      this.syncButtons();
      this.toast(
        `Comedy SFX Pacer: ${this.sfxEngine.pacerEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"}`
      );
    });

    this.els.muteBtn?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      await this.sfxEngine.resume();
      this.sfxEngine.setMuted(!this.sfxEngine.muted);
      this.syncButtons();
    });

    this.els.panelToggleBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      this.els.studioPanel?.classList.toggle("collapsed");
    });

    // Tab switching
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-tab]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        this.activeTab = btn.dataset.tab;
        document.querySelectorAll("[data-tab]").forEach((b) => {
          b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.tab === this.activeTab);
        });
        document.querySelectorAll("[data-tab-panel]").forEach((p) => {
          p.hidden = p.dataset.tabPanel !== this.activeTab;
        });
      });
    });

    // Quick Director buttons
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-char-anim]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        await this.sfxEngine.resume();
        const [actorId, animName] = btn.dataset.charAnim.split(":");
        const actor = this.characters[actorId];
        if (!actor) return;
        actor.root.visible = true;
        actor.playAnim(animName, 0.16, null, animName !== "jump" && animName !== "punch");
        this.sfxEngine.notifyAction(animName, this.runner.simTime);
      });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll("[data-char-bubble]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        await this.sfxEngine.resume();
        const [actorId, icon] = btn.dataset.charBubble.split(":");
        const actor = this.characters[actorId];
        if (!actor) return;
        actor.showBubble(icon, 2.2);
        this.sfxEngine.notifyBubblePop(icon, this.runner.simTime);
      });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll("[data-player-gesture]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        this.playerCamera.triggerGesture(btn.dataset.playerGesture, 0.85);
      });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll("[data-player-hold]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        await this.sfxEngine.resume();
        const prop = btn.dataset.playerHold || null;
        this.playerCamera.holdProp(prop === "none" ? null : prop);
        if (prop && prop !== "none") {
          this.sfxEngine.notifyPickup(prop, this.runner.simTime);
        }
      });
    });

    document.getElementById("stopBgmBtn")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      this.sfxEngine.stopBgm(this.runner.simTime);
    });

    this.els.applyJsonBtn?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      await this.sfxEngine.resume();
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(this.els.episodeEditor.value);
        this.onLoadEpisode(parsed);
        this.runner.play();
        this.toast("Applied & playing custom skit JSON!");
      } catch (err) {
        this.toast(`JSON Error: ${err.message}`, true);
      }
    });

    this.els.copyCoordBtn?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      const txt = this.els.coordReadout?.textContent || "";
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);
        this.toast("Copied waypoint coordinates!");
      } catch {
        this.toast(txt);
      }
    });

    this.els.exportBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      this.exportWebm();
    });
  }

  populatePresets() {
    if (!this.els.presetSelect) return;
    this.els.presetSelect.innerHTML = "";
    for (const [key, ep] of Object.entries(EPISODE_PRESETS)) {
      const opt = document.createElement("option");
      opt.value = key;
      opt.textContent = ep.title;
      this.els.presetSelect.appendChild(opt);
    }
    this.els.episodeEditor.value = JSON.stringify(
      EPISODE_PRESETS.grandpa_key_pov_44s,
      null,
      2
    );
  }

  populateSoundboard() {
    if (!this.els.soundboardGrid) return;
    this.els.soundboardGrid.innerHTML = "";
    for (const [key, meta] of Object.entries(SFX_MANIFEST)) {
      const btn = document.createElement("button");
      btn.type = "button";
      btn.className = "sfx-btn";
      btn.textContent = key;
      btn.title = `${meta.category.toUpperCase()} (${meta.path})`;
      btn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        await this.sfxEngine.resume();
        if (meta.category === "bgm") {
          this.sfxEngine.playBgm(key, { volume: meta.defaultVolume });
        } else {
          this.sfxEngine.play(key, { cooldown: 0.05 });
        }
      });
      this.els.soundboardGrid.appendChild(btn);
    }
  }

  populateWaypoints() {
    if (!this.els.waypointList) return;
    this.els.waypointList.innerHTML = "";
    for (const [name, wp] of Object.entries(SET_MAP)) {
      const btn = document.createElement("button");
      btn.type = "button";
      btn.className = "wp-btn";
      btn.innerHTML = `<strong>${name}</strong><span>(${wp.x.toFixed(1)}, ${wp.y.toFixed(1)}, ${wp.z.toFixed(1)})</span>`;
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        this.playerCamera.reset(wp, null);
        this.playerCamera.orbitControls?.target?.set(wp.x, wp.y + 1.1, wp.z - 1.5);
        this.toast(`Moved to "${name}"`);
      });
      this.els.waypointList.appendChild(btn);
    }
  }

  setEpisodeJson(episode) {
    if (this.els.episodeEditor) {
      this.els.episodeEditor.value = JSON.stringify(episode, null, 2);
    }
  }

  syncState({ simTime, duration, isPlaying }) {
    if (this.els.timelineSlider) {
      this.els.timelineSlider.max = String(Math.max(0.1, duration));
      this.els.timelineSlider.value = String(simTime);
    }
    if (this.els.timeReadout) {
      this.els.timeReadout.textContent = `${simTime.toFixed(1)}s / ${duration.toFixed(1)}s`;
    }
    if (this.els.playPauseBtn) {
      this.els.playPauseBtn.textContent = isPlaying ? "Pause" : "Play";
    }
    if (this.els.fadeOverlay && this.playerCamera) {
      this.els.fadeOverlay.style.opacity = String(this.playerCamera.fadeAlpha);
    }
    if (this.els.coordReadout && this.playerCamera) {
      const p = this.playerCamera.camera.position;
      this.els.coordReadout.textContent = `{ "x": ${p.x.toFixed(2)}, "y": ${(p.y - CONFIG.playerEyeHeight).toFixed(2)}, "z": ${p.z.toFixed(2)} }`;
    }
  }

  syncButtons() {
    if (this.els.aspectToggleBtn) {
      this.els.aspectToggleBtn.textContent = `Aspect: ${this.playerCamera.aspectKey}`;
    }
    if (this.els.camModeBtn) {
      this.els.camModeBtn.textContent =
        this.playerCamera.mode === "pov" ? "Cam: POV" : "Cam: Orbit";
    }
    if (this.els.pacerToggleBtn) {
      this.els.pacerToggleBtn.textContent = `SFX Pacer: ${this.sfxEngine.pacerEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"}`;
      this.els.pacerToggleBtn.classList.toggle("active", this.sfxEngine.pacerEnabled);
    }
    if (this.els.muteBtn) {
      this.els.muteBtn.textContent = this.sfxEngine.muted ? "Unmute" : "Mute";
    }
  }

  toast(msg, isError = false) {
    if (!this.els.statusToast) return;
    this.els.statusToast.textContent = msg;
    this.els.statusToast.classList.toggle("error", isError);
    this.els.statusToast.classList.add("visible");
    clearTimeout(this._toastTimer);
    this._toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.els.statusToast?.classList.remove("visible");
    }, 2800);
  }

  async exportWebm() {
    if (this.isRecording) return;
    if (!window.MediaRecorder) {
      this.toast("MediaRecorder unsupported — use Cloud Render workflow!", true);
      return;
    }

    await this.sfxEngine.resume();
    this.isRecording = true;
    this.onExportStateChange?.(true);
    this.els.exportBtn.disabled = true;
    this.els.exportBtn.textContent = "Recording...";

    try {
      const canvas = this.renderer.domElement;
      const videoStream = canvas.captureStream(CONFIG.exportFps);
      const audioStream = this.sfxEngine.getMediaStream();
      const combined = new MediaStream([
        ...videoStream.getVideoTracks(),
        ...audioStream.getAudioTracks(),
      ]);

      const mimeType = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported("video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus")
        ? "video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus"
        : "video/webm";

      const chunks = [];
      const recorder = new MediaRecorder(combined, {
        mimeType,
        videoBitsPerSecond: 6_000_000,
      });

      recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
        if (e.data && e.data.size > 0) chunks.push(e.data);
      };

      const donePromise = new Promise((resolve) => {
        recorder.onstop = resolve;
      });

      this.runner.reset();
      recorder.start(250);
      this.runner.play();

      const prevOnFinish = this.runner.onFinish;
      await new Promise((resolve) => {
        this.runner.onFinish = () => {
          prevOnFinish?.();
          resolve();
        };
      });
      this.runner.onFinish = prevOnFinish;

      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 250));
      recorder.stop();
      await donePromise;

      const blob = new Blob(chunks, { type: mimeType });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = url;
      a.download = "granny-pov-episode.webm";
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      this.toast("Exported granny-pov-episode.webm!");
    } catch (err) {
      this.toast(`Export failed: ${err.message}`, true);
    } finally {
      this.isRecording = false;
      this.onExportStateChange?.(false);
      this.els.exportBtn.disabled = false;
      this.els.exportBtn.textContent = "Record WebM";
    }
  }
}
